- [ ] **Step 4: Write `packages/core/src/crm/record-ref.ts`**

```ts
import { and, asc, db, eq, isNull, type SQL, schema } from '@gravity/db';
import { notFound, validationFailed } from '@gravity/shared/errors';
import { assertCan, type Principal } from '@gravity/shared/policy';
import { normalizeDomain, normalizeLinkedinProfileUrl, parseLeadKey } from '@gravity/shared/utils';
import { arrayOverlaps } from 'drizzle-orm';
import { getLeadByKey } from './lead-service.ts';

export type RecordRefType = 'person' | 'company' | 'lead';

export interface ResolvedRecordRef {
  readonly type: RecordRefType;
  readonly id: string;
}

const EMAIL = /^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/;
const SHOWN_REF_LENGTH = 80;

function nothingMatches(ref: string) {
  return notFound(
    `Nothing in this workspace matches "${ref.slice(0, SHOWN_REF_LENGTH)}". Use an email, a LinkedIn profile URL, a domain, a lead key like ABC-12, or an id.`,
  );
}

async function personWhere(organizationId: string, condition: SQL): Promise<string | null> {
  const [row] = await db
    .select({ id: schema.person.id })
    .from(schema.person)
    .where(and(eq(schema.person.organizationId, organizationId), isNull(schema.person.archivedAt), condition))
    .orderBy(asc(schema.person.createdAt), asc(schema.person.id))
    .limit(1);
  return row?.id ?? null;
}

async function companyWhere(organizationId: string, condition: SQL): Promise<string | null> {
  const [row] = await db
    .select({ id: schema.company.id })
    .from(schema.company)
    .where(and(eq(schema.company.organizationId, organizationId), isNull(schema.company.archivedAt), condition))
    .orderBy(asc(schema.company.createdAt), asc(schema.company.id))
    .limit(1);
  return row?.id ?? null;
}

async function leadWhere(organizationId: string, leadId: string): Promise<string | null> {
  const [row] = await db
    .select({ id: schema.lead.id })
    .from(schema.lead)
    .innerJoin(schema.pipeline, eq(schema.pipeline.id, schema.lead.pipelineId))
    .where(
      and(
        eq(schema.lead.organizationId, organizationId),
        eq(schema.lead.id, leadId),
        isNull(schema.lead.archivedAt),
        isNull(schema.pipeline.archivedAt),
      ),
    )
    .limit(1);
  return row?.id ?? null;
}

async function recordById(organizationId: string, id: string): Promise<ResolvedRecordRef | null> {
  const person = await personWhere(organizationId, eq(schema.person.id, id));
  if (person !== null) return { type: 'person', id: person };
  const company = await companyWhere(organizationId, eq(schema.company.id, id));
  if (company !== null) return { type: 'company', id: company };
  const lead = await leadWhere(organizationId, id);
  return lead === null ? null : { type: 'lead', id: lead };
}

function found(type: RecordRefType, id: string | null, ref: string): ResolvedRecordRef {
  if (id === null) throw nothingMatches(ref);
  return { type, id };
}

export async function resolveRecordRef(principal: Principal, raw: string): Promise<ResolvedRecordRef> {
  assertCan(principal, 'record:read');
  const ref = raw.trim();
  if (ref.length === 0) {
    throw validationFailed('Name a record by email, LinkedIn URL, domain, lead key or id.');
  }
  const organizationId = principal.organizationId;
  if (parseLeadKey(ref) !== null) {
    return { type: 'lead', id: (await getLeadByKey(principal, ref)).id };
  }
  const linkedinUrl = normalizeLinkedinProfileUrl(ref);
  if (linkedinUrl !== null) {
    return found('person', await personWhere(organizationId, eq(schema.person.linkedinUrl, linkedinUrl)), ref);
  }
  if (EMAIL.test(ref)) {
    return found(
      'person',
      await personWhere(organizationId, arrayOverlaps(schema.person.emails, [ref.toLowerCase()])),
      ref,
    );
  }
  const domain = ref.includes('.') && !/\s/.test(ref) ? normalizeDomain(ref) : null;
  if (domain !== null) {
    return found('company', await companyWhere(organizationId, arrayOverlaps(schema.company.domains, [domain])), ref);
  }
  const byId = await recordById(organizationId, ref);
  if (byId === null) throw nothingMatches(ref);
  return byId;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Write `packages/core/src/crm/context-service.ts`**

```ts
import { leadPriorityLabel } from '@gravity/shared/constants';
import { assertCan, type Principal } from '@gravity/shared/policy';
import type {
  ActivityRow,
  CompanyRow,
  EmploymentRow,
  LeadRow,
  PersonRow,
} from '@gravity/shared/records';
import type { RecordLinks } from '@gravity/shared/utils';
import { listMembers } from '../org/member-service.ts';
import { listTimeline } from './activity-service.ts';
import { getLead } from './lead-service.ts';
import { listPipelines } from './pipeline-service.ts';
import type { ResolvedRecordRef } from './record-ref.ts';
import { getCompanyRecord, getPersonRecord } from './record-service.ts';
import { listStages } from './stage-service.ts';

const TIMELINE_LIMIT = 50;
const CHARS_PER_TOKEN = 4;

export interface ContextNames {
  readonly stages: Readonly<Record<string, string>>;
  readonly pipelines: Readonly<Record<string, string>>;
  readonly members: Readonly<Record<string, string>>;
}

export interface RecordContext {
  readonly subject: ResolvedRecordRef;
  readonly person: PersonRow | null;
  readonly company: CompanyRow | null;
  readonly focusLeadId: string | null;
  readonly employments: readonly EmploymentRow[];
  readonly people: readonly { readonly person: PersonRow; readonly employment: EmploymentRow }[];
  readonly leads: readonly LeadRow[];
  readonly timeline: readonly ActivityRow[];
  readonly names: ContextNames;
}

async function contextNames(principal: Principal): Promise<ContextNames> {
  const [stages, pipelines, members] = await Promise.all([
    listStages(principal),
    listPipelines(principal),
    listMembers(principal),
  ]);
  return {
    stages: Object.fromEntries(stages.map((stage) => [stage.id, stage.name])),
    pipelines: Object.fromEntries(pipelines.map((pipeline) => [pipeline.id, pipeline.name])),
    members: Object.fromEntries(members.map((entry) => [entry.user.id, entry.user.name])),
  };
}

export async function getRecordContext(
  principal: Principal,
  subject: ResolvedRecordRef,
): Promise<RecordContext> {
  assertCan(principal, 'record:read');
  const names = await contextNames(principal);
  if (subject.type === 'company') {
    const record = await getCompanyRecord(principal, subject.id);
    const timeline = await listTimeline(principal, {
      subjectType: 'company',
      subjectId: subject.id,
      limit: TIMELINE_LIMIT,
    });
    return {
      subject,
      person: null,
      company: record.company,
      focusLeadId: null,
      employments: [],
      people: record.people,
      leads: record.leads,
      timeline: timeline.activities,
      names,
    };
  }
  const lead = subject.type === 'lead' ? await getLead(principal, subject.id) : null;
  const personId = lead?.personId ?? subject.id;
  const record = await getPersonRecord(principal, personId);
  const timeline = await listTimeline(principal, {
    subjectType: 'person',
    subjectId: personId,
    limit: TIMELINE_LIMIT,
  });
  return {
    subject,
    person: record.person,
    company: null,
    focusLeadId: lead?.id ?? null,
    employments: record.employments,
    people: [],
    leads: record.leads,
    timeline: timeline.activities,
    names,
  };
}

function present<T>(value: T | null | undefined): value is T {
  return value !== null && value !== undefined && value !== '';
}

function fieldsLine(fields: Readonly<Record<string, unknown>>): string[] {
  const entries = Object.entries(fields).map(([key, value]) =>
    `${key}=${Array.isArray(value) ? value.map(String).join('|') : String(value)}`,
  );
  return entries.length === 0 ? [] : [`Fields: ${entries.join('; ')}`];
}

function personLines(context: RecordContext, person: PersonRow, links: RecordLinks): string[] {
  return [
    `Person: ${person.name}${person.primaryEmail === null ? '' : ` <${person.primaryEmail}>`}`,
    `Link: ${links.person(person.id)}`,
    ...(present(person.title) || present(person.companyName)
      ? [`Works as: ${[person.title, person.companyName].filter(present).join(' at ')}`]
      : []),
    ...(person.emails.length > 1 ? [`Emails: ${person.emails.join(', ')}`] : []),
    ...(present(person.linkedinUrl) ? [`LinkedIn: ${person.linkedinUrl}`] : []),
    ...(person.phones.length > 0 ? [`Phones: ${person.phones.join(', ')}`] : []),
    ...(present(person.location) || present(person.timezone)
      ? [`Location: ${[person.location, person.timezone].filter(present).join(' · ')}`]
      : []),
    `Do not contact: ${person.doNotContact ? 'yes' : 'no'}`,
    ...fieldsLine(person.fields),
    ...(context.employments.length === 0
      ? []
      : [
          'Jobs:',
          ...context.employments.map(
            (job) =>
              `- ${job.title ?? 'Role unknown'} at ${job.companyName}${job.isCurrent ? ' (current)' : job.endedAt === null ? '' : ` (until ${job.endedAt})`}`,
          ),
        ]),
  ];
}

function companyLines(context: RecordContext, company: CompanyRow, links: RecordLinks): string[] {
  return [
    `Company: ${company.name}`,
    `Link: ${links.company(company.id)}`,
    ...(company.domains.length > 0 ? [`Domains: ${company.domains.join(', ')}`] : []),
    ...([company.size, company.segment, company.location].some(present)
      ? [`About: ${[company.size, company.segment, company.location].filter(present).join(' · ')}`]
      : []),
    ...fieldsLine(company.fields),
    ...(context.people.length === 0
      ? []
      : [
          'People:',
          ...context.people.map(
            (entry) => `- ${entry.person.name}${entry.employment.title === null ? '' : `, ${entry.employment.title}`} ${links.person(entry.person.id)}`,
          ),
        ]),
  ];
}

function leadLines(context: RecordContext, links: RecordLinks): string[] {
  if (context.leads.length === 0) return ['Leads: none'];
  const names = context.names;
  return [
    'Leads:',
    ...context.leads.map((lead) => {
      const parts = [
        `${lead.key}${lead.id === context.focusLeadId ? ' (in focus)' : ''}`,
        ...(context.company === null ? [] : [lead.personName]),
        names.pipelines[lead.pipelineId] ?? 'Unknown pipeline',
        `stage ${names.stages[lead.stageId] ?? lead.stageId} (${lead.stageCategory})`,
        `owner ${lead.ownerId === null ? 'nobody' : (names.members[lead.ownerId] ?? 'a former member')}`,
        `priority ${leadPriorityLabel(lead.priority)}`,
        ...(lead.nextAction === null
          ? []
          : [`next: ${lead.nextAction}${lead.nextActionAt === null ? '' : ` by ${lead.nextActionAt.slice(0, 10)}`}`]),
        ...(lead.holdReason === null ? [] : [`on hold: ${lead.holdReason}`]),
        links.lead(lead.key),
      ];
      return `- ${parts.join(' · ')}`;
    }),
  ];
}

function textOf(payload: Record<string, unknown>, key: string): string | null {
  const value = payload[key];
  return typeof value === 'string' && value.length > 0 ? value : null;
}

function changedKeys(payload: Record<string, unknown>): string[] {
  const changes = payload['changes'];
  return typeof changes === 'object' && changes !== null && !Array.isArray(changes) ? Object.keys(changes) : [];
}

function activityLine(activity: ActivityRow, names: ContextNames): string {
  const actor =
    activity.actor.name ??
    (activity.actor.type === 'user' ? names.members[activity.actor.id] : undefined) ??
    (activity.actor.type === 'system' ? 'Gravity' : activity.actor.id);
  const subject = textOf(activity.payload, 'key') ?? textOf(activity.payload, 'name');
  const changes = changedKeys(activity.payload);
  return `${activity.occurredAt.slice(0, 10)} ${actor}: ${activity.kind}${subject === null ? '' : ` ${subject}`}${changes.length === 0 ? '' : ` (${changes.join(', ')})`}`;
}

export interface RenderContextOptions {
  readonly maxTokens: number;
  readonly links: RecordLinks;
}

export function renderRecordContext(context: RecordContext, options: RenderContextOptions): string {
  const head =
    context.person !== null
      ? personLines(context, context.person, options.links)
      : context.company !== null
        ? companyLines(context, context.company, options.links)
        : [];
  const leads = leadLines(context, options.links);
  const timeline = context.timeline.map((activity) => activityLine(activity, context.names));
  const budget = options.maxTokens * CHARS_PER_TOKEN;
  const compose = (count: number) =>
    [
      ...head,
      ...leads,
      ...(count === 0 ? [] : ['Recent activity:', ...timeline.slice(0, count).map((line) => `- ${line}`)]),
      ...(count < timeline.length
        ? [`(${timeline.length - count} older entries left out to fit ${options.maxTokens} tokens)`]
        : []),
    ].join('\n');
  let count = timeline.length;
  let text = compose(count);
  while (text.length > budget && count > 0) {
    count -= 1;
    text = compose(count);
  }
  if (text.length <= budget) return text;
  const notice = `\n(truncated to fit ${options.maxTokens} tokens)`;
  return `${text.slice(0, Math.max(0, budget - notice.length))}${notice}`;
}
```

### Task 8: MCP read tools `search`, `get_context` and `list_leads`

The M0 done-when: "an agent can search and get context over MCP". Each tool is a thin adapter over the core read service the REST routes use, called with the grant's principal, so tenant isolation and role checks are shared. Errors name the field and list the valid values (spec 7.2).

**Files:**
- Create: `packages/mcp-server/src/tools/records.ts`
- Modify: `packages/mcp-server/src/tools/index.ts`
- Test: `packages/mcp-server/tests/tools/records.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `searchRecords`, `listLeads`, `listPipelines`, `listStages`, `listMembers`, `listSavedViews`, `listFieldDefinitions` (M0.2); `resolveRecordRef`, `getRecordContext`, `renderRecordContext` (Task 7); `CONTEXT_TOKENS`, `leadPriorityLabel`, `SEARCH_RESULT_LIMIT` (`@gravity/shared/constants`); `leadFilterRegistry` (`@gravity/shared/filters`); `defineTool`, `ToolContext` (Task 6).
- Produces: tools `search { query: string; limit?: number }`, `get_context { ref: string; max_tokens?: number }`, `list_leads { pipeline: string; filter?: object; view?: string; query?: string; cursor?: string; limit?: number }`; `registerRecordTools(server: McpServer, context: ToolContext): void`.

```ts
import { afterAll, beforeAll, describe, expect, test } from 'bun:test';
import {
  closeRealtime,
  createBrand,
  createLead,
  createOrganization,
  createSavedView,
  resolvePrincipal,
  upsertPerson,
} from '@gravity/core';
import { addMember, createWorkspace, mintMcpToken, resetDatabase, type TestWorkspace } from '@gravity/core/test-support';
import { connect, type TestClient } from '../../src/test-helpers.ts';

let workspace: TestWorkspace;
let client: TestClient;
let otherWorkspaceId = '';
let otherPersonId = '';
let readyId = '';
let personId = '';

beforeAll(async () => {
  await resetDatabase();
  workspace = await createWorkspace('Nimbus');
  const context = { principal: workspace.admin };
  const brand = await createBrand(context, { name: 'Lumen', pipelineKey: 'LUM' });
  readyId = brand.stages.find((stage) => stage.name === 'Ready')?.id ?? '';
  const ada = await upsertPerson(context, {
    name: 'Ada Lovelace',
    emails: ['ada@vela.example'],
    company: { domain: 'vela.example', name: 'Vela Robotics' },
  });
  personId = ada.person.id;
  const grace = await upsertPerson(context, { name: 'Grace Hopper', emails: ['grace@quarry.example'] });
  await createLead(context, { personId, pipelineId: brand.pipeline.id });
  await createLead(context, { personId: grace.person.id, pipelineId: brand.pipeline.id, stageId: readyId });
  await createSavedView(context, {
    object: 'lead',
    pipelineId: brand.pipeline.id,
    name: 'Ready ones',
    visibility: 'workspace',
    filter: {
      kind: 'group',
      combinator: 'and',
      children: [{ kind: 'condition', property: 'stage', operator: 'in', values: [readyId] }],
    },
  });
  const other = await createOrganization(workspace.adminUser.id, { name: 'Elsewhere', slug: 'elsewhere-mcp' });
  otherWorkspaceId = other.organization.id;
  const elsewhere = await resolvePrincipal(workspace.adminUser.id, otherWorkspaceId);
  await createBrand({ principal: elsewhere }, { name: 'Other', pipelineKey: 'OTH' });
  otherPersonId = (
    await upsertPerson({ principal: elsewhere }, { name: 'Bea Byron', emails: ['bea@other.example'] })
  ).person.id;
  client = await connect((await mintMcpToken(workspace.organizationId, workspace.adminUser.id)).token);
});

afterAll(async () => {
  await client.close();
  await closeRealtime();
});

describe('search', () => {
  test('finds people, companies and leads with links', async () => {
    const { text, data } = await client.result('search', { query: 'Ada' });
    expect(data['people']).toEqual([
      expect.objectContaining({ name: 'Ada Lovelace', url: `http://localhost:3300/people/${personId}` }),
    ]);
    expect(text).toContain('Ada Lovelace');
  });
});

describe('get_context', () => {
  test('answers for an email, a lead key and a domain', async () => {
    const byEmail = await client.result('get_context', { ref: 'ada@vela.example' });
    expect(byEmail.text).toContain('Person: Ada Lovelace <ada@vela.example>');
    expect(byEmail.data['subject']).toEqual({
      type: 'person',
      id: personId,
      url: `http://localhost:3300/people/${personId}`,
    });
    const byKey = await client.result('get_context', { ref: 'LUM-1' });
    expect(byKey.data['subject']).toMatchObject({ type: 'lead', url: 'http://localhost:3300/l/LUM-1' });
    expect(byKey.text).toContain('LUM-1 (in focus)');
    const byDomain = await client.result('get_context', { ref: 'vela.example' });
    expect(byDomain.text).toContain('Company: Vela Robotics');
  });
});

describe('list_leads', () => {
  test('lists a pipeline by key with the filter language, a saved view and paging', async () => {
    const all = await client.result('list_leads', { pipeline: 'lum' });
    expect((all.data['leads'] as { key: string }[]).map((lead) => lead.key)).toEqual(['LUM-2', 'LUM-1']);
    const filtered = await client.result('list_leads', {
      pipeline: 'LUM',
      filter: {
        kind: 'group',
        combinator: 'and',
        children: [{ kind: 'condition', property: 'stage', operator: 'in', values: [readyId] }],
      },
    });
    expect((filtered.data['leads'] as { person: string; stage: string }[])).toEqual([
      expect.objectContaining({ person: 'Grace Hopper', stage: 'Ready' }),
    ]);
    const viewed = await client.result('list_leads', { pipeline: 'LUM', view: 'ready ones' });
    expect((viewed.data['leads'] as { key: string }[]).map((lead) => lead.key)).toEqual(['LUM-2']);
    const first = await client.result('list_leads', { pipeline: 'LUM', limit: 1 });
    const cursor = first.data['nextCursor'];
    expect(typeof cursor).toBe('string');
    const second = await client.result('list_leads', { pipeline: 'LUM', limit: 1, cursor: String(cursor) });
    expect((second.data['leads'] as { key: string }[]).map((lead) => lead.key)).toEqual(['LUM-1']);
  });

  test('names the valid properties and pipelines when the request is wrong', async () => {
    const property = await client.failure('list_leads', {
      pipeline: 'LUM',
      filter: {
        kind: 'group',
        combinator: 'and',
        children: [{ kind: 'condition', property: 'ghost', operator: 'in', values: ['x'] }],
      },
    });
    expect(property.code).toBe('validation_failed');
    expect(property.message).toContain('Filter properties: stage, stageCategory, owner');
    const pipeline = await client.failure('list_leads', { pipeline: 'NOPE' });
    expect(pipeline).toEqual({ code: 'not_found', message: 'There is no pipeline NOPE. Pipelines: LUM.' });
  });
});

describe('workspace boundary', () => {
  test('every tool stays inside the grant workspace, even for a user who belongs to both', async () => {
    expect((await client.result('search', { query: 'Byron' })).data['people']).toEqual([]);
    expect((await client.failure('get_context', { ref: 'bea@other.example' })).code).toBe('not_found');
    expect((await client.failure('get_context', { ref: otherPersonId })).code).toBe('not_found');
    expect((await client.failure('list_leads', { pipeline: 'OTH' })).code).toBe('not_found');
    const elsewhere = await connect((await mintMcpToken(otherWorkspaceId, workspace.adminUser.id)).token);
    try {
      expect((await elsewhere.result('search', { query: 'Byron' })).data['people']).toEqual([
        expect.objectContaining({ name: 'Bea Byron' }),
      ]);
    } finally {
      await elsewhere.close();
    }
  });

  test('a guest can read', async () => {
    const guest = await addMember(workspace, 'Gus Guest', 'guest');
    const guestClient = await connect((await mintMcpToken(workspace.organizationId, guest.userId, 'gravity.read')).token);
    try {
      expect((await guestClient.result('search', { query: 'Grace' })).data['people']).toHaveLength(1);
    } finally {
      await guestClient.close();
    }
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write `packages/mcp-server/src/tools/records.ts`**

```ts
import type { McpServer } from '@modelcontextprotocol/sdk/server/mcp.js';
import {
  getRecordContext,
  listFieldDefinitions,
  listLeads,
  listMembers,
  listPipelines,
  listSavedViews,
  listStages,
  renderRecordContext,
  resolveRecordRef,
  searchRecords,
} from '@gravity/core';
import { CONTEXT_TOKENS, leadPriorityLabel, SEARCH_RESULT_LIMIT } from '@gravity/shared/constants';
import { isDomainError, notFound, validationFailed } from '@gravity/shared/errors';
import { type FilterGroup, leadFilterRegistry } from '@gravity/shared/filters';
import type { Principal } from '@gravity/shared/policy';
import type { LeadRow, PipelineRow, SavedViewRow } from '@gravity/shared/records';
import type { RecordLinks } from '@gravity/shared/utils';
import { z } from 'zod';
import type { ToolContext } from './index.ts';
import { defineTool } from './support.ts';

const LIST_LIMIT = 50;

interface Names {
  readonly stages: ReadonlyMap<string, string>;
  readonly members: ReadonlyMap<string, string>;
}

async function namesFor(principal: Principal): Promise<Names> {
  const [stages, members] = await Promise.all([listStages(principal), listMembers(principal)]);
  return {
    stages: new Map(stages.map((stage) => [stage.id, stage.name])),
    members: new Map(members.map((entry) => [entry.user.id, entry.user.name])),
  };
}

function leadView(lead: LeadRow, names: Names, links: RecordLinks) {
  return {
    key: lead.key,
    url: links.lead(lead.key),
    person: lead.personName,
    email: lead.personEmail,
    company: lead.companyName,
    stage: names.stages.get(lead.stageId) ?? lead.stageId,
    stageCategory: lead.stageCategory,
    owner: lead.ownerId === null ? null : (names.members.get(lead.ownerId) ?? null),
    ownerId: lead.ownerId,
    priority: leadPriorityLabel(lead.priority),
    nextAction: lead.nextAction,
    nextActionAt: lead.nextActionAt,
    holdReason: lead.holdReason,
    updatedAt: lead.updatedAt,
  };
}

function leadLine(view: ReturnType<typeof leadView>): string {
  return [
    `- ${view.key} ${view.person}${view.company === null ? '' : ` (${view.company})`}`,
    view.stage,
    view.owner ?? 'no owner',
    view.priority,
    ...(view.nextAction === null ? [] : [`next: ${view.nextAction}`]),
    view.url,
  ].join(' · ');
}

function pipelineOf(pipelines: readonly PipelineRow[], ref: string): PipelineRow {
  const pipeline = pipelines.find((entry) => entry.id === ref || entry.key === ref.toUpperCase());
  if (pipeline !== undefined) return pipeline;
  const keys = pipelines.map((entry) => entry.key).join(', ');
  throw notFound(`There is no pipeline ${ref}. Pipelines: ${keys.length === 0 ? 'none yet' : keys}.`);
}

function leadViewOf(views: readonly SavedViewRow[], ref: string, pipeline: PipelineRow): SavedViewRow {
  const view = views.find((entry) => entry.id === ref || entry.name.toLowerCase() === ref.toLowerCase());
  if (view === undefined) {
    const names = views.filter((entry) => entry.object === 'lead').map((entry) => entry.name);
    throw notFound(`There is no saved view ${ref}. Lead views: ${names.length === 0 ? 'none yet' : names.join(', ')}.`);
  }
  if (view.object !== 'lead') throw validationFailed(`${view.name} is a ${view.object} view; list_leads takes lead views.`);
  if (view.pipelineId !== null && view.pipelineId !== pipeline.id) {
    throw validationFailed(`${view.name} belongs to another pipeline.`);
  }
  return view;
}

function combinedFilter(
  viewFilter: FilterGroup | undefined,
  raw: Record<string, unknown> | undefined,
): unknown {
  if (viewFilter !== undefined && raw !== undefined) {
    return { kind: 'group', combinator: 'and', children: [viewFilter, raw] };
  }
  return viewFilter ?? raw;
}

async function filterProperties(principal: Principal, pipelineId: string): Promise<string[]> {
  const fields = await listFieldDefinitions(principal);
  const leadFields = fields.filter(
    (field) => field.object === 'lead' && (field.pipelineId === null || field.pipelineId === pipelineId),
  );
  return leadFilterRegistry(leadFields, pipelineId).properties.map((property) => property.key);
}

export function registerRecordTools(server: McpServer, context: ToolContext): void {
  const { principal, links } = context;

  defineTool(
    server,
    {
      name: 'search',
      title: 'Search people, companies and leads',
      description:
        'Fuzzy search by name, email, domain or lead key such as ABC-12. Returns people, companies and leads, each with a link.',
      scope: 'read',
      inputSchema: {
        query: z.string().trim().min(1).max(200).describe('Words, an email, a domain or a lead key.'),
        limit: z.number().int().min(1).max(20).optional().describe('Results per kind, 8 by default.'),
      },
    },
    async (args) => {
      const result = await searchRecords(principal, { q: args.query, limit: args.limit ?? SEARCH_RESULT_LIMIT });
      const people = result.people.map((person) => ({
        id: person.id,
        name: person.name,
        email: person.primaryEmail,
        company: person.companyName,
        title: person.title,
        url: links.person(person.id),
      }));
      const companies = result.companies.map((company) => ({
        id: company.id,
        name: company.name,
        domain: company.primaryDomain,
        url: links.company(company.id),
      }));
      const leads = result.leads.map((lead) => ({
        key: lead.key,
        person: lead.personName,
        company: lead.companyName,
        stageCategory: lead.stageCategory,
        url: links.lead(lead.key),
      }));
      const lines = [
        `People (${people.length}):`,
        ...people.map((person) => `- ${person.name}${person.email === null ? '' : ` <${person.email}>`}${person.company === null ? '' : `, ${person.company}`} ${person.url}`),
        `Companies (${companies.length}):`,
        ...companies.map((company) => `- ${company.name}${company.domain === null ? '' : ` (${company.domain})`} ${company.url}`),
        `Leads (${leads.length}):`,
        ...leads.map((lead) => `- ${lead.key} ${lead.person} [${lead.stageCategory}] ${lead.url}`),
      ];
      return { text: lines.join('\n'), data: { people, companies, leads } };
    },
  );

  defineTool(
    server,
    {
      name: 'get_context',
      title: 'Get context for one record',
      description:
        'A compact, token-budgeted bundle for a person, company or lead: attributes, custom fields, jobs, every lead with owner and stage, and recent activity. Name the record by email, LinkedIn URL, domain, lead key such as ABC-12, or id.',
      scope: 'read',
      inputSchema: {
        ref: z.string().trim().min(1).max(500).describe('Email, LinkedIn profile URL, domain, lead key or id.'),
        max_tokens: z
          .number()
          .int()
          .min(CONTEXT_TOKENS.min)
          .max(CONTEXT_TOKENS.max)
          .optional()
          .describe('Upper bound for the text, 2000 by default.'),
      },
    },
    async (args) => {
      const subject = await resolveRecordRef(principal, args.ref);
      const bundle = await getRecordContext(principal, subject);
      const names = await namesFor(principal);
      const focus = bundle.leads.find((lead) => lead.id === subject.id);
      const url =
        subject.type === 'person'
          ? links.person(subject.id)
          : subject.type === 'company'
            ? links.company(subject.id)
            : links.lead(focus?.key ?? '');
      return {
        text: renderRecordContext(bundle, { maxTokens: args.max_tokens ?? CONTEXT_TOKENS.default, links }),
        data: {
          subject: { ...subject, url },
          person: bundle.person,
          company: bundle.company,
          employments: bundle.employments,
          people: bundle.people.map((entry) => ({ ...entry, url: links.person(entry.person.id) })),
          leads: bundle.leads.map((lead) => leadView(lead, names, links)),
          timeline: bundle.timeline.slice(0, 20),
        },
      };
    },
  );

  defineTool(
    server,
    {
      name: 'list_leads',
      title: 'List leads in a pipeline',
      description:
        'Leads in one pipeline, newest first, using the same filter language as the web app (see describe_workspace for stage, member and field ids) and optionally a saved lead view by id or name. Pass nextCursor back as cursor for the next page.',
      scope: 'read',
      inputSchema: {
        pipeline: z.string().trim().min(1).max(64).describe('Pipeline key such as ABC, or its id.'),
        filter: z.record(z.string(), z.unknown()).optional().describe('A filter group.'),
        view: z.string().trim().min(1).max(200).optional().describe('A saved lead view id or name.'),
        query: z.string().trim().max(200).optional().describe('Free text over person, email, company and key.'),
        cursor: z.string().max(2048).optional(),
        limit: z.number().int().min(1).max(100).optional().describe('Page size, 50 by default.'),
      },
    },
    async (args) => {
      const [pipelines, views, names] = await Promise.all([
        listPipelines(principal),
        listSavedViews(principal),
        namesFor(principal),
      ]);
      const pipeline = pipelineOf(pipelines, args.pipeline);
      const view = args.view === undefined ? null : leadViewOf(views, args.view, pipeline);
      const filter = combinedFilter(view?.filter, args.filter);
      let page: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof listLeads>>;
      try {
        page = await listLeads(principal, {
          pipelineId: pipeline.id,
          filter,
          q: args.query ?? '',
          ...(args.cursor === undefined ? {} : { cursor: args.cursor }),
          limit: args.limit ?? LIST_LIMIT,
        });
      } catch (error: unknown) {
        if (filter !== undefined && isDomainError(error) && error.code === 'validation_failed') {
          const properties = await filterProperties(principal, pipeline.id);
          throw validationFailed(
            `${error.message} Filter properties: ${properties.join(', ')}.`,
            error.details === undefined ? {} : { details: error.details },
          );
        }
        throw error;
      }
      const leads = page.leads.map((lead) => leadView(lead, names, links));
      const heading = `${pipeline.key} ${pipeline.name}: ${leads.length} lead${leads.length === 1 ? '' : 's'}${page.nextCursor === null ? '' : ', more with the next cursor'}`;
      return {
        text: [heading, ...leads.map(leadLine)].join('\n'),
        data: {
          pipeline: { id: pipeline.id, key: pipeline.key, name: pipeline.name, url: links.pipeline(pipeline.key) },
          leads,
          nextCursor: page.nextCursor,
        },
      };
    },
  );
}
```

In `packages/mcp-server/src/tools/index.ts` import `registerRecordTools` from `./records.ts` and call `registerRecordTools(server, context);` after `registerWorkspaceTools`.

### Task 9: Copy for agent (`Cmd+Shift+A`)

UI spec 5: every record page has "Copy for agent" (`Cmd+Shift+A`), which copies the same text `get_context` returns, so a person can paste a record into an agent chat. M0.2 deferred it to this milestone.

**Files:**
- Modify: `packages/shared/src/validators/lists.ts`
- Create: `apps/web/src/app/api/context/route.ts`
- Create: `apps/web/src/features/records/use-copy-for-agent.ts`
- Modify: `apps/web/src/features/records/record-layout.tsx`
- Test: `apps/web/tests/app/api/context/route.test.ts`, `apps/web/tests/features/records/use-copy-for-agent.test.tsx`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `resolveRecordRef`, `getRecordContext`, `renderRecordContext` (Task 7); `CONTEXT_TOKENS` (Task 7); `recordLinks` (Task 6); `publicAppUrl` (Task 4); `handle`, `searchParamsOf` (`@/lib/api/handler.ts`); `apiFetch`, `messageOf` (`@/lib/api/client.ts`); `useHotkey`; `useToast`.
- Produces:
  - `contextQuerySchema` (`{ ref: string; maxTokens: number }`) in `@gravity/shared/validators`.
  - `GET /api/context?ref=<ref>&maxTokens=<n>` answering `{ subject: { type, id }, text }`.
  - `COPY_FOR_AGENT_BINDING = 'mod+shift+a'`, `useCopyForAgent(recordId: string): void`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

`apps/web/tests/app/api/context/route.test.ts`:

```ts
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, test } from 'bun:test';
import { upsertPerson } from '@gravity/core';
import { createWorkspace, resetDatabase, type TestWorkspace } from '@gravity/core/test-support';
import { GET } from '@/app/api/context/route.ts';
import { signedInAs } from '../../../../tests-support.ts';

let workspace: TestWorkspace;
let personId = '';

beforeEach(async () => {
  await resetDatabase();
  workspace = await createWorkspace();
  personId = (
    await upsertPerson({ principal: workspace.admin }, { name: 'Ada Lovelace', emails: ['ada@vela.example'] })
  ).person.id;
  await signedInAs(workspace.adminUser.id, workspace.organizationId);
});

describe('/api/context', () => {
  test('returns the agent text for a record id', async () => {
    const response = await GET(new Request(`http://localhost:3300/api/context?ref=${personId}`));
    expect(response.status).toBe(200);
    const body = (await response.json()) as { subject: unknown; text: string };
    expect(body.subject).toEqual({ type: 'person', id: personId });
    expect(body.text).toContain('Person: Ada Lovelace <ada@vela.example>');
    expect(body.text).toContain(`http://localhost:3300/people/${personId}`);
  });

  test('refuses a token budget outside the bounds and a record of another workspace', async () => {
    expect((await GET(new Request(`http://localhost:3300/api/context?ref=${personId}&maxTokens=10`))).status).toBe(422);
    const other = await createWorkspace('Other');
    await signedInAs(other.adminUser.id, other.organizationId);
    expect((await GET(new Request(`http://localhost:3300/api/context?ref=${personId}`))).status).toBe(404);
  });
});
```

`apps/web/tests/features/records/use-copy-for-agent.test.tsx`:

```tsx
import { describe, expect, mock, test } from 'bun:test';
import { screen, waitFor } from '@testing-library/react';
import userEvent from '@testing-library/user-event';
import { useCopyForAgent } from '@/features/records/use-copy-for-agent.ts';
import { serveJson } from '../../support/fetch.ts';
import { renderWithClient } from '../../support/render.tsx';

function Record() {
  useCopyForAgent('per1');
  return null;
}

function stubClipboard(writeText: (text: string) => Promise<void>): void {
  Object.defineProperty(navigator, 'clipboard', { configurable: true, value: { writeText } });
}

describe('useCopyForAgent', () => {
  test('Cmd+Shift+A copies the context text of the record', async () => {
    const writeText = mock((_text: string) => Promise.resolve());
    stubClipboard(writeText);
    const sent = serveJson(() => ({
      body: { subject: { type: 'person', id: 'per1' }, text: 'Person: Ada Lovelace' },
    }));
    renderWithClient(<Record />);
    await userEvent.keyboard('{Meta>}{Shift>}a{/Shift}{/Meta}');
    await waitFor(() => expect(writeText).toHaveBeenCalledWith('Person: Ada Lovelace'));
    expect(sent[0]?.url).toBe('/api/context?ref=per1');
    expect(await screen.findByText('Context copied')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  test('names the failure when the server refuses', async () => {
    stubClipboard(() => Promise.resolve());
    serveJson(() => ({
      status: 404,
      body: { error: { code: 'not_found', message: 'That person does not exist.' } },
    }));
    renderWithClient(<Record />);
    await userEvent.keyboard('{Meta>}{Shift>}a{/Shift}{/Meta}');
    expect(await screen.findByText('Could not copy the context')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('That person does not exist.')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });
});
```

```ts
export const contextQuerySchema = z.object({
  ref: z.string().trim().min(1).max(500),
  maxTokens: z.coerce
    .number()
    .int()
    .min(CONTEXT_TOKENS.min)
    .max(CONTEXT_TOKENS.max)
    .default(CONTEXT_TOKENS.default),
});
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write the route**

`apps/web/src/app/api/context/route.ts`:

```ts
import { getRecordContext, renderRecordContext, resolveRecordRef } from '@gravity/core';
import { recordLinks } from '@gravity/shared/utils';
import { contextQuerySchema } from '@gravity/shared/validators';
import { handle, searchParamsOf } from '@/lib/api/handler.ts';
import { publicAppUrl } from '@/lib/env.ts';

export const dynamic = 'force-dynamic';

export async function GET(request: Request): Promise<Response> {
  return await handle(async (principal) => {
    const query = contextQuerySchema.parse(searchParamsOf(request));
    const subject = await resolveRecordRef(principal, query.ref);
    const context = await getRecordContext(principal, subject);
    return {
      subject,
      text: renderRecordContext(context, {
        maxTokens: query.maxTokens,
        links: recordLinks(publicAppUrl()),
      }),
    };
  });
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Write the hook and use it on record pages**

`apps/web/src/features/records/use-copy-for-agent.ts`:

```ts
'use client';

import { useCallback } from 'react';
import { z } from 'zod';
import { useToast } from '@/components/ui/toast.tsx';
import { apiFetch, messageOf } from '@/lib/api/client.ts';
import { useHotkey } from '@/lib/keyboard/index.ts';

export const COPY_FOR_AGENT_BINDING = 'mod+shift+a';

const contextResponseSchema = z.object({
  subject: z.object({ type: z.enum(['person', 'company', 'lead']), id: z.string() }),
  text: z.string(),
});

export function useCopyForAgent(recordId: string): void {
  const { toast } = useToast();
  const copy = useCallback(async () => {
    try {
      const { text } = await apiFetch(
        `/api/context?ref=${encodeURIComponent(recordId)}`,
        contextResponseSchema,
      );
      await navigator.clipboard.writeText(text);
      toast({ title: 'Context copied', description: 'Paste it into your agent chat.' });
    } catch (error: unknown) {
      toast({
        title: 'Could not copy the context',
        description: messageOf(error, 'The browser blocked clipboard access.'),
        tone: 'danger',
      });
    }
  }, [recordId, toast]);
  useHotkey(
    COPY_FOR_AGENT_BINDING,
    () => {
      copy().catch(() => undefined);
    },
    { label: 'Copy this record for an agent', section: 'Records', allowInInput: true },
  );
}
```

### Task 10: MCP clients settings with revocation

Spec 6.1 lists "MCP clients" under Settings. Each member sees the MCP connections they authorized (client, workspace, scopes, connected and last used) and can revoke one; revocation deletes the connection's tokens, so the client's next call gets 401 and must reconnect. The page also shows the server URL to paste into an MCP client.

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/web/src/app/api/mcp-grants/route.ts`, `apps/web/src/app/api/mcp-grants/[id]/route.ts`
- Create: `apps/web/src/app/(app)/settings/mcp/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/features/settings/mcp-panel.tsx`
- Modify: `apps/web/src/features/settings/settings-nav.tsx`
- Test: `apps/web/tests/app/api/mcp-grants/route.test.ts`, `apps/web/tests/features/settings/mcp-panel.test.tsx`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `listMcpGrants`, `revokeMcpGrant`, `mintMcpToken` (Task 3); `scopeList`, `GRAVITY_SCOPES` (Task 3); `mcpServerUrl` (Task 4); `apiContext`, `handleRoute`, `routeId` (`@/lib/api/handler.ts`).
- Produces:
  - `GET /api/mcp-grants` answering `{ connections: { id, clientName, organizationName, scopes: string[], createdAt, lastUsedAt }[] }` for the signed-in user only.
  - `DELETE /api/mcp-grants/[id]` answering `{ ok: true }`, or 404 for a connection that is not the caller's.
  - `McpPanel({ serverUrl }: { serverUrl: string })`; query key `['mcp-grants']` (not persisted).

```ts
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, test } from 'bun:test';
import { addMember, createWorkspace, mintMcpToken, resetDatabase, type TestWorkspace } from '@gravity/core/test-support';
import { db, schema } from '@gravity/db';
import { DELETE } from '@/app/api/mcp-grants/[id]/route.ts';
import { GET } from '@/app/api/mcp-grants/route.ts';
import { signedInAs } from '../../../../tests-support.ts';

let workspace: TestWorkspace;

beforeEach(async () => {
  await resetDatabase();
  workspace = await createWorkspace('Nimbus');
});

function remove(id: string): Promise<Response> {
  return DELETE(new Request(`http://localhost:3300/api/mcp-grants/${id}`, { method: 'DELETE' }), {
    params: Promise.resolve({ id }),
  });
}

describe('/api/mcp-grants', () => {
  test('lists only the caller connections and revokes one with its tokens', async () => {
    const mine = await mintMcpToken(workspace.organizationId, workspace.adminUser.id, 'openid gravity.read');
    const teammate = await addMember(workspace, 'Tess Teammate', 'member');
    await mintMcpToken(workspace.organizationId, teammate.userId);
    await signedInAs(workspace.adminUser.id, workspace.organizationId);
    const listed = (await (await GET()).json()) as { connections: { id: string; scopes: string[]; organizationName: string }[] };
    expect(listed.connections).toEqual([
      expect.objectContaining({ id: mine.grantId, scopes: ['openid', 'gravity.read'], organizationName: 'Nimbus' }),
    ]);
    expect((await remove(mine.grantId)).status).toBe(200);
    expect(((await (await GET()).json()) as { connections: unknown[] }).connections).toEqual([]);
    expect(await db.select().from(schema.oauthAccessToken)).toHaveLength(1);
  });

  test('a teammate cannot revoke my connection', async () => {
    const mine = await mintMcpToken(workspace.organizationId, workspace.adminUser.id);
    const teammate = await addMember(workspace, 'Tess Teammate', 'member');
    await signedInAs(teammate.userId, workspace.organizationId);
    expect((await remove(mine.grantId)).status).toBe(404);
  });
});
```

```tsx
import { describe, expect, test } from 'bun:test';
import { screen, waitFor, within } from '@testing-library/react';
import userEvent from '@testing-library/user-event';
import { McpPanel } from '@/features/settings/mcp-panel.tsx';
import { serveJson } from '../../support/fetch.ts';
import { renderWithClient } from '../../support/render.tsx';

const CONNECTION = {
  id: 'g1',
  clientName: 'Desk agent',
  organizationName: 'Acme Studio',
  scopes: ['openid', 'gravity.read'],
  createdAt: '2026-10-01T10:00:00.000Z',
  lastUsedAt: null,
};

describe('McpPanel', () => {
  test('shows the server URL and each connection with its scopes', async () => {
    serveJson(() => ({ body: { connections: [CONNECTION] } }));
    renderWithClient(<McpPanel serverUrl="http://localhost:3300/mcp" />);
    expect(screen.getByText('http://localhost:3300/mcp')).toBeInTheDocument();
    const row = await screen.findByRole('listitem', { name: 'Desk agent' });
    expect(within(row).getByText('Acme Studio')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(within(row).getByText('gravity.read')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(within(row).getByText('Never used')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  test('revokes after confirming and removes the row', async () => {
    let revoked = false;
    const sent = serveJson((url, method) => {
      if (method === 'DELETE') {
        revoked = true;
        return { body: { ok: true } };
      }
      return { body: { connections: revoked ? [] : [CONNECTION] } };
    });
    renderWithClient(<McpPanel serverUrl="http://localhost:3300/mcp" />);
    await userEvent.click(await screen.findByRole('button', { name: 'Revoke Desk agent' }));
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Revoke access' }));
    await waitFor(() => expect(screen.queryByRole('listitem', { name: 'Desk agent' })).toBeNull());
    expect(sent.some((entry) => entry.method === 'DELETE' && entry.url === '/api/mcp-grants/g1')).toBe(true);
  });

  test('says how to connect when there are no connections', async () => {
    serveJson(() => ({ body: { connections: [] } }));
    renderWithClient(<McpPanel serverUrl="http://localhost:3300/mcp" />);
    expect(await screen.findByText('No agents are connected yet.')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });
});
```

`apps/web/src/app/api/mcp-grants/route.ts`:

```ts
import { listMcpGrants } from '@gravity/core';
import { scopeList } from '@gravity/shared/constants';
import { apiContext, handleRoute } from '@/lib/api/handler.ts';

export const dynamic = 'force-dynamic';

export async function GET(): Promise<Response> {
  return await handleRoute(async () => {
    const { principal } = await apiContext();
    const grants = await listMcpGrants(principal.userId);
    return {
      connections: grants.map((grant) => ({
        id: grant.id,
        clientName: grant.clientName,
        organizationName: grant.organizationName,
        scopes: scopeList(grant.scopes),
        createdAt: grant.createdAt.toISOString(),
        lastUsedAt: grant.lastUsedAt?.toISOString() ?? null,
      })),
    };
  });
}
```

`apps/web/src/app/api/mcp-grants/[id]/route.ts`:

```ts
import { revokeMcpGrant } from '@gravity/core';
import { apiContext, handleRoute, routeId } from '@/lib/api/handler.ts';

export const dynamic = 'force-dynamic';

export async function DELETE(
  _request: Request,
  { params }: { readonly params: Promise<{ id: string }> },
): Promise<Response> {
  return await handleRoute(async () => {
    const { principal } = await apiContext();
    await revokeMcpGrant(routeId((await params).id, 'connection'), principal.userId);
    return { ok: true };
  });
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write the panel and the page**

`apps/web/src/features/settings/mcp-panel.tsx`:

```tsx
'use client';

import { useMutation, useQuery, useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { Copy, PlugZap } from 'lucide-react';
import { useState } from 'react';
import { z } from 'zod';
import { Badge } from '@/components/ui/badge.tsx';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button.tsx';
import {
  Dialog,
  DialogContent,
  DialogDescription,
  DialogFooter,
  DialogHeader,
  DialogTitle,
} from '@/components/ui/dialog.tsx';
import { EmptyState } from '@/components/ui/empty-state.tsx';
import { ErrorState } from '@/components/ui/error-state.tsx';
import { ListSkeleton } from '@/components/ui/list-skeleton.tsx';
import { RelativeTime } from '@/components/ui/relative-time.tsx';
import { useToast } from '@/components/ui/toast.tsx';
import { apiFetch, messageOf } from '@/lib/api/client.ts';
import { cn } from '@/lib/cn.ts';
import { rowHover } from '@/lib/interaction.ts';
import { useDelayedFlag } from '@/lib/use-delayed-flag.ts';

const connectionSchema = z.object({
  id: z.string(),
  clientName: z.string(),
  organizationName: z.string(),
  scopes: z.array(z.string()),
  createdAt: z.string(),
  lastUsedAt: z.string().nullable(),
});
type Connection = z.infer<typeof connectionSchema>;
const connectionsSchema = z.object({ connections: z.array(connectionSchema) });
const MCP_GRANTS_KEY = ['mcp-grants'] as const;
const GRAVITY_PREFIX = 'gravity.';

export function McpPanel({ serverUrl }: { readonly serverUrl: string }) {
  const client = useQueryClient();
  const { toast } = useToast();
  const [confirming, setConfirming] = useState<Connection | null>(null);
  const connections = useQuery({
    queryKey: MCP_GRANTS_KEY,
    queryFn: () => apiFetch('/api/mcp-grants', connectionsSchema),
  });
  const loading = useDelayedFlag(connections.isPending);
  const revoke = useMutation({
    mutationFn: (connection: Connection) =>
      apiFetch(`/api/mcp-grants/${connection.id}`, z.object({ ok: z.literal(true) }), { method: 'DELETE' }),
    onSuccess: (_result, connection) => {
      client.setQueryData(MCP_GRANTS_KEY, (current: z.infer<typeof connectionsSchema> | undefined) =>
        current === undefined
          ? current
          : { connections: current.connections.filter((entry) => entry.id !== connection.id) },
      );
      toast({ title: `Revoked ${connection.clientName}`, description: 'It must reconnect to use Gravity again.' });
    },
    onError: (error, connection) => {
      toast({
        title: `Could not revoke ${connection.clientName}`,
        description: messageOf(error),
        tone: 'danger',
        action: { label: 'Retry', onSelect: () => revoke.mutate(connection) },
      });
    },
  });

  function copyUrl(): void {
    navigator.clipboard
      .writeText(serverUrl)
      .then(() => toast({ title: 'Server URL copied' }))
      .catch(() => toast({ title: 'Could not copy the URL', tone: 'danger' }));
  }

  return (
    <div className="mx-auto flex w-full max-w-3xl flex-col gap-6 px-6 py-6">
      <header className="flex flex-col gap-1">
        <h1 className="font-medium text-lg text-text">MCP clients</h1>
        <p className="text-dense text-muted">
          Agents connect over OAuth. Add this server to any MCP client and approve it for one
          workspace.
        </p>
      </header>
      <div className="flex h-9 items-center gap-2 rounded-md border border-border bg-surface-2 px-3">
        <code className="min-w-0 flex-1 truncate font-mono text-dense text-text">{serverUrl}</code>
        <Button size="sm" variant="ghost" onClick={copyUrl} aria-label="Copy server URL">
          <Copy className="size-3.5" aria-hidden="true" />
        </Button>
      </div>
      {connections.error !== null ? (
        <ErrorState
          title="Could not load your MCP clients"
          error={connections.error}
          onRetry={() => {
            connections.refetch().catch(() => undefined);
          }}
        />
      ) : connections.data === undefined ? (
        loading ? <ListSkeleton /> : null
      ) : connections.data.connections.length === 0 ? (
        <EmptyState
          icon={<PlugZap />}
          title="No agents are connected yet."
          description="Paste the server URL into your MCP client and approve it here."
        />
      ) : (
        <ul className="flex flex-col">
          {connections.data.connections.map((connection) => (
            <li
              key={connection.id}
              aria-label={connection.clientName}
              className={cn('flex h-7 items-center gap-3 rounded-md px-2 text-dense', rowHover)}
            >
              <span className="w-40 truncate font-medium text-text">{connection.clientName}</span>
              <span className="w-32 truncate text-muted">{connection.organizationName}</span>
              <span className="flex min-w-0 flex-1 gap-1">
                {connection.scopes
                  .filter((scope) => scope.startsWith(GRAVITY_PREFIX))
                  .map((scope) => (
                    <Badge key={scope} tone={scope === 'gravity.approve' ? 'warning' : 'neutral'}>
                      {scope}
                    </Badge>
                  ))}
              </span>
              <span className="w-28 text-faint text-xs">
                {connection.lastUsedAt === null ? 'Never used' : <RelativeTime at={connection.lastUsedAt} />}
              </span>
              <Button
                size="sm"
                variant="ghost"
                aria-label={`Revoke ${connection.clientName}`}
                onClick={() => setConfirming(connection)}
              >
                Revoke
              </Button>
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>
      )}
      <Dialog open={confirming !== null} onOpenChange={(open) => (open ? undefined : setConfirming(null))}>
        <DialogContent>
          <DialogHeader>
            <DialogTitle>Revoke {confirming?.clientName}?</DialogTitle>
            <DialogDescription>
              Its tokens stop working immediately. It can connect again only after you approve it.
            </DialogDescription>
          </DialogHeader>
          <DialogFooter>
            <Button variant="ghost" onClick={() => setConfirming(null)}>
              Cancel
            </Button>
            <Button
              variant="danger"
              onClick={() => {
                if (confirming !== null) revoke.mutate(confirming);
                setConfirming(null);
              }}
            >
              Revoke access
            </Button>
          </DialogFooter>
        </DialogContent>
      </Dialog>
    </div>
  );
}
```

### Task 11: Pure import engine in `@gravity/shared/import`

Everything about a file that does not need the database: parsing CSV (RFC 4180 quoting, BOM, CRLF, comma, semicolon or tab) and JSON (an array of flat objects), the caps, the column vocabulary, mapping suggestions from headers, coercion of text into custom field values, planning each row into a `PersonInput`, `CompanyInput` and lead draft with row-level issues, and in-file dedupe. The browser runs it for the instant mapping screen and the server runs the same code before it touches a row.

**Files:**
- Create: `packages/shared/src/import/index.ts`, `constants.ts`, `table.ts`, `mapping.ts`, `coerce.ts`, `plan.ts`, `request.ts`, `report.ts` (all under `packages/shared/src/import/`)
- Modify: `packages/shared/package.json` (export `./import`)
- Test: `packages/shared/tests/import/table.test.ts`, `mapping.test.ts`, `coerce.test.ts`, `plan.test.ts`, `request.test.ts`, `report.test.ts` (all under `packages/shared/tests/import/`)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `personInputSchema`, `companyInputSchema`, `PersonInput`, `CompanyInput`, `CompanyRef`, `fieldValueSchema`, `FieldDefinitionLike`, `FieldValue`, `idSchema` (`@gravity/shared/validators`); `LEAD_PRIORITIES`, `LEAD_PRIORITY_LABELS`, `StageCategory` (`@gravity/shared/constants`); `payloadTooLarge`, `unsupportedMediaType`, `validationFailed` (`@gravity/shared/errors`).
- Produces (`@gravity/shared/import`):
  - Constants: `IMPORT_FORMATS`, `ImportFormat`, `IMPORT_TARGETS`, `ImportTarget`, `IMPORT_TARGET_LABELS`, `IMPORT_DEFAULT_OWNERS`, `ImportDefaultOwner`, `MAX_IMPORT_BYTES`, `MAX_IMPORT_ROWS`, `MAX_CLI_IMPORT_ROWS`, `MAX_IMPORT_COLUMNS`, `MAX_IMPORT_CELL_LENGTH`, `IMPORT_CHUNK_ROWS = 100`, `IMPORT_PREVIEW_CHUNK_ROWS = 500`, `interface ImportLimits { maxBytes: number; maxRows: number }`, `HTTP_IMPORT_LIMITS`, `CLI_IMPORT_LIMITS`.
  - Table: `interface ImportTable { headers: readonly string[]; rows: readonly (readonly string[])[] }`, `parseCsv(content, limits): ImportTable`, `parseJsonRecords(content, limits): ImportTable`, `parseImportTable(format: ImportFormat, content: string, limits: ImportLimits): ImportTable`, `utf8Length(text: string): number`.
  - Mapping: `IMPORT_STANDARD_COLUMNS`, `ImportStandardColumn`, `ImportFieldObject = 'person' | 'company' | 'lead'`, `ImportColumn`, `customColumn(object, key): ImportColumn`, `isImportColumn(value: string): value is ImportColumn`, `customFieldOf(column): { object: ImportFieldObject; key: string } | null`, `importColumnSchema`, `importMappingSchema`, `ImportMapping = Record<string, ImportColumn>`, `columnAllowed(target, column): boolean`, `importColumnLabel(column): string`, `mappingIssues(mapping, target, headers, fieldKeys): string[]`, `suggestMapping(headers, target, definitions: readonly { object: string; key: string; label: string }[]): ImportMapping`.
  - Coerce: `booleanOf(raw: string): boolean | null`, `type Coerced`, `coerceFieldValue(definition: FieldDefinitionLike, raw: string): Coerced`.
  - Plan: `ImportIssue`, `ImportMember`, `ImportStage`, `ImportSetup`, `PlannedLead`, `PlannedRow`, `planImport(table: ImportTable, mapping: ImportMapping, setup: ImportSetup): PlannedRow[]`.
  - Request: `IMPORT_SOURCE_PATTERN`, `importRequestSchema`, `ImportRequest`.
  - Report: `IMPORT_ROW_STATUSES`, `IMPORT_COMPANY_OUTCOMES`, `IMPORT_LEAD_OUTCOMES`, `IMPORT_MATCHES`, `importIssueSchema`, `importRowOutcomeSchema`, `ImportRowOutcome`, `importTotalsSchema`, `ImportTotals`, `importReportSchema`, `ImportReport`, `totalsOf(rows: readonly ImportRowOutcome[], planned: number): ImportTotals`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

`packages/shared/tests/import/table.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, expect, test } from 'bun:test';
import { HTTP_IMPORT_LIMITS, MAX_IMPORT_CELL_LENGTH } from '../../src/import/constants.ts';
import { parseCsv, parseImportTable, parseJsonRecords } from '../../src/import/table.ts';

const limits = HTTP_IMPORT_LIMITS;

describe('parseCsv', () => {
  test('handles a BOM, CRLF, quoted delimiters, quoted newlines and escaped quotes', () => {
    const table = parseCsv('﻿Name,Note\r\n"Lovelace, Ada","Line one\nline ""two"""\r\nGrace,plain\r\n', limits);
    expect(table.headers).toEqual(['Name', 'Note']);
    expect(table.rows).toEqual([
      ['Lovelace, Ada', 'Line one\nline "two"'],
      ['Grace', 'plain'],
    ]);
  });

  test('detects a semicolon export and skips blank lines', () => {
    const table = parseCsv('Name;Email\n\nAda;ada@vela.example\n;\n', limits);
    expect(table.rows).toEqual([['Ada', 'ada@vela.example']]);
  });

  test('keeps a formula-looking cell verbatim', () => {
    expect(parseCsv('Name\n=HYPERLINK("x")\n', limits).rows).toEqual([['=HYPERLINK("x")']]);
  });

  test('names empty headers and separates duplicates', () => {
    expect(parseCsv('Email,,Email\na,b,c\n', limits).headers).toEqual(['Email', 'Column 2', 'Email (2)']);
  });

  test('refuses an unterminated quote, a NUL byte, an empty file and a header without rows', () => {
    expect(() => parseCsv('Name\n"Ada\n', limits)).toThrow(
      'The file ends inside a quoted value that starts on line 2.',
    );
    expect(() => parseCsv('Name\nA\u0000da\n', limits)).toThrow('This does not look like a text file.');
    expect(() => parseCsv('\n\n', limits)).toThrow('This file is empty.');
    expect(() => parseCsv('Name\n', limits)).toThrow('This file has a header but no rows.');
  });

  test('enforces the byte, row, column and cell caps', () => {
    expect(() => parseCsv(`Name\n${'a'.repeat(30)}\n`, { maxBytes: 20, maxRows: 10 })).toThrow(
      /Import files up to/,
    );
    expect(() => parseCsv('Name\na\nb\nc\n', { maxBytes: 1000, maxRows: 2 })).toThrow(
      'This file has 3 rows. Import at most 2 at a time, or split the file.',
    );
    const wide = Array.from({ length: 101 }, (_, index) => `c${index}`).join(',');
    expect(() => parseCsv(`${wide}\n${wide}\n`, limits)).toThrow('This file has 101 columns. Import at most 100.');
    expect(() => parseCsv(`Name\n${'x'.repeat(MAX_IMPORT_CELL_LENGTH + 1)}\n`, limits)).toThrow(
      'Row 1, column Name is longer than 5000 characters.',
    );
  });
});

describe('parseJsonRecords', () => {
  test('unions keys in first-seen order and flattens lists and scalars', () => {
    const table = parseJsonRecords(
      JSON.stringify([
        { name: 'Ada', emails: ['a@x.example', 'b@x.example'] },
        { name: 'Grace', vip: true, score: 3, email: null },
      ]),
      limits,
    );
    expect(table.headers).toEqual(['name', 'emails', 'vip', 'score', 'email']);
    expect(table.rows).toEqual([
      ['Ada', 'a@x.example; b@x.example', '', '', ''],
      ['Grace', '', 'true', '3', ''],
    ]);
  });

  test('refuses nested values, non-arrays and invalid JSON', () => {
    expect(() => parseJsonRecords('[{"name":"Ada","company":{"name":"Vela"}}]', limits)).toThrow(
      'Row 1, key company holds a nested value.',
    );
    expect(() => parseJsonRecords('{"name":"Ada"}', limits)).toThrow(
      'A JSON import is an array of objects, one per row.',
    );
    expect(() => parseJsonRecords('[{', limits)).toThrow('This file is not valid JSON.');
  });

  test('parseImportTable picks the parser by format', () => {
    expect(parseImportTable('json', '[{"a":"1"}]', limits).headers).toEqual(['a']);
    expect(parseImportTable('csv', 'a\n1\n', limits).rows).toEqual([['1']]);
  });
});
```

```ts
import { describe, expect, test } from 'bun:test';
import {
  importMappingSchema,
  mappingIssues,
  suggestMapping,
} from '../../src/import/mapping.ts';

const definitions = [
  { object: 'person', key: 'seniority', label: 'Seniority' },
  { object: 'lead', key: 'deal_size', label: 'Deal size' },
  { object: 'deal', key: 'ignored', label: 'Ignored' },
];
const NO_FIELDS = { person: [], company: [], lead: [] };

describe('suggestMapping', () => {
  test('maps common headers and custom field labels for each target', () => {
    const headers = ['Full Name', 'Work Email', 'E-mail', 'Company', 'Website', 'Job Title', 'LinkedIn URL', 'Stage', 'Seniority', 'Deal size', 'Notes'];
    expect(suggestMapping(headers, 'people', definitions)).toEqual({
      'Full Name': 'person.name',
      'Work Email': 'person.email',
      'E-mail': 'person.email',
      Company: 'company.name',
      Website: 'company.domain',
      'Job Title': 'person.title',
      'LinkedIn URL': 'person.linkedinUrl',
      Stage: 'ignore',
      Seniority: 'person.field.seniority',
      'Deal size': 'ignore',
      Notes: 'ignore',
    });
    const leads = suggestMapping(headers, 'leads', definitions);
    expect(leads['Stage']).toBe('lead.stage');
    expect(leads['Deal size']).toBe('lead.field.deal_size');
    expect(suggestMapping(['Name', 'Location'], 'companies', definitions)).toEqual({
      Name: 'ignore',
      Location: 'company.location',
    });
  });
});

describe('mappingIssues', () => {
  test('names every problem a mapping can have', () => {
    expect(
      mappingIssues(
        { Ghost: 'person.name', Stage: 'lead.stage', Tier: 'person.field.tier', A: 'person.title', B: 'person.title' },
        'people',
        ['Stage', 'Tier', 'A', 'B'],
        NO_FIELDS,
      ),
    ).toEqual([
      'The file has no column called Ghost.',
      'Lead stage cannot be imported into people.',
      'There is no person field called tier.',
      'B and A both map to Job title. Map each field once.',
    ]);
    expect(mappingIssues({ Email: 'person.email' }, 'people', ['Email'], NO_FIELDS)).toEqual([
      'Map a name column, or first and last name columns.',
    ]);
    expect(
      mappingIssues({ First: 'person.firstName', A: 'person.email', B: 'person.email' }, 'people', ['First', 'A', 'B'], NO_FIELDS),
    ).toEqual([]);
    expect(mappingIssues({ Size: 'company.size' }, 'companies', ['Size'], NO_FIELDS)).toEqual([
      'Map a company name or domain column.',
    ]);
  });

  test('the mapping schema accepts custom columns and refuses unknown ones', () => {
    expect(importMappingSchema.safeParse({ A: 'lead.field.deal_size' }).success).toBe(true);
    expect(importMappingSchema.safeParse({ A: 'person.secret' }).success).toBe(false);
  });
});
```

```ts
import { describe, expect, test } from 'bun:test';
import { booleanOf, coerceFieldValue } from '../../src/import/coerce.ts';

const select = {
  key: 'seniority',
  type: 'select' as const,
  options: [
    { value: 'junior', label: 'Junior' },
    { value: 'senior', label: 'Senior' },
  ],
};

describe('coerceFieldValue', () => {
  test('turns text into each field type', () => {
    expect(coerceFieldValue({ key: 'n', type: 'number', options: [] }, '1,200')).toEqual({ ok: true, value: 1200 });
    expect(coerceFieldValue({ key: 'b', type: 'boolean', options: [] }, 'Yes')).toEqual({ ok: true, value: true });
    expect(coerceFieldValue({ key: 'd', type: 'date', options: [] }, '2031-03-04')).toEqual({ ok: true, value: '2031-03-04' });
    expect(coerceFieldValue(select, 'Senior')).toEqual({ ok: true, value: 'senior' });
    expect(coerceFieldValue({ ...select, type: 'multi_select' }, 'junior; Senior')).toEqual({
      ok: true,
      value: ['junior', 'senior'],
    });
  });

  test('explains what does not fit', () => {
    expect(coerceFieldValue({ key: 'n', type: 'number', options: [] }, 'many')).toEqual({ ok: false, message: 'many is not a number.' });
    expect(coerceFieldValue({ key: 'b', type: 'boolean', options: [] }, 'maybe')).toEqual({
      ok: false,
      message: 'Use yes or no, not maybe.',
    });
    const refused = coerceFieldValue(select, 'Principal');
    expect(refused.ok).toBe(false);
    expect(refused.ok === false ? refused.message : '').toBe('Pick one of: junior, senior.');
  });

  test('booleanOf reads common spellings', () => {
    expect([booleanOf('y'), booleanOf('FALSE'), booleanOf('0'), booleanOf('sure')]).toEqual([true, false, false, null]);
  });
});
```

```ts
import { describe, expect, test } from 'bun:test';
import { HTTP_IMPORT_LIMITS } from '../../src/import/constants.ts';
import type { ImportMapping } from '../../src/import/mapping.ts';
import { type ImportSetup, planImport } from '../../src/import/plan.ts';
import { parseCsv } from '../../src/import/table.ts';

const setup: ImportSetup = {
  target: 'leads',
  pipelineId: 'p1',
  stages: [
    { id: 's-new', name: 'New', category: 'open' },
    { id: 's-ready', name: 'Ready', category: 'open' },
    { id: 's-hold', name: 'On hold', category: 'hold' },
    { id: 's-lost', name: 'Closed: no reply', category: 'lost' },
  ],
  members: [{ userId: 'u1', name: 'Ada Admin', email: 'ada@acme.test' }],
  fields: {
    person: [{ key: 'seniority', type: 'select', options: [{ value: 'senior', label: 'Senior' }] }],
    company: [],
    lead: [],
  },
  defaultOwnerId: 'u2',
};

const MAPPING: ImportMapping = {
  First: 'person.firstName',
  Last: 'person.lastName',
  Email: 'person.email',
  LinkedIn: 'person.linkedinUrl',
  Company: 'company.name',
  Domain: 'company.domain',
  Title: 'person.title',
  Stage: 'lead.stage',
  Owner: 'lead.owner',
  Priority: 'lead.priority',
  Next: 'lead.nextActionAt',
  Seniority: 'person.field.seniority',
};

function plan(csv: string, target: ImportSetup['target'] = 'leads') {
  return planImport(parseCsv(csv, HTTP_IMPORT_LIMITS), MAPPING, { ...setup, target });
}

const HEADER = 'First,Last,Email,LinkedIn,Company,Domain,Title,Stage,Owner,Priority,Next,Seniority\n';

describe('planImport', () => {
  test('plans a person, the company and the lead of a clean row', () => {
    const [row] = plan(`${HEADER}Ada,Lovelace,ADA@Vela.example,linkedin.com/in/Ada,Vela Robotics,www.vela.example,CTO,ready,ADA@acme.test,high,2031-03-04,Senior\n`);
    expect(row?.issues).toEqual([]);
    expect(row?.label).toBe('Ada Lovelace');
    expect(row?.person).toMatchObject({
      name: 'Ada Lovelace',
      emails: ['ada@vela.example'],
      linkedinUrl: 'https://www.linkedin.com/in/ada',
      title: 'CTO',
      company: { domain: 'vela.example', name: 'Vela Robotics' },
      fields: { seniority: 'senior' },
    });
    expect(row?.company).toMatchObject({ name: 'Vela Robotics', domains: ['vela.example'] });
    expect(row?.lead).toEqual({
      stageId: 's-ready',
      stageCategory: 'open',
      ownerId: 'u1',
      priority: 2,
      nextAction: null,
      nextActionAt: '2031-03-04T00:00:00.000Z',
      fields: {},
    });
  });

  test('uses the default owner and the first open stage when the columns are empty', () => {
    const [row] = plan(`${HEADER}Grace,Hopper,grace@quarry.example,,,,,,,,,\n`);
    expect(row?.lead).toMatchObject({ stageId: undefined, ownerId: 'u2', priority: 0 });
  });

  test('reports each bad value with its column and blocks the row', () => {
    const [row] = plan(`${HEADER},,not-an-email,,,,,Limbo,nobody@acme.test,sometimes,someday,Principal\n`);
    expect(row?.person).toBeNull();
    expect(row?.issues.map((issue) => [issue.column, issue.message])).toEqual([
      [null, 'This row has no name.'],
      ['Seniority', 'Pick one of: senior.'],
      ['Stage', 'There is no stage called Limbo. Stages: New, Ready, Closed: no reply.'],
      ['Owner', "No member matches nobody@acme.test. Use a member's email."],
      ['Priority', 'Use a priority from 0 to 4 or a label such as Urgent or Low, not sometimes.'],
      ['Next', 'Use a date like 2031-03-04, not someday.'],
    ]);
  });

  test('refuses a hold stage as a starting stage', () => {
    const [row] = plan(`${HEADER}Ada,L,a@v.example,,,,,On hold,,,,\n`);
    expect(row?.issues.map((issue) => issue.message)).toEqual([
      'Leads cannot start in On hold. Import them into an open stage, then put them on hold with a reason.',
    ]);
  });

  test('marks a later row with an earlier identity as a duplicate but never name-only rows', () => {
    const rows = plan(
      `${HEADER}Ada,L,ada@vela.example,,,,,,,,,\nAda,Again,ADA@vela.example,,,,,,,,,\nNo,Email,,,,,,,,,,\nNo,Email,,,,,,,,,,\n`,
    );
    expect(rows.map((row) => row.duplicateOf)).toEqual([null, 1, null, null]);
  });

  test('flags cells beyond the header', () => {
    const [row] = plan(`${HEADER}Ada,L,a@v.example,,,,,,,,,,surprise\n`);
    expect(row?.issues[0]?.message).toBe('This row has 13 cells but the header has 12.');
  });

  test('plans companies on their own and dedupes them by domain', () => {
    const rows = planImport(
      parseCsv('Name,Domain,Size\nVela,vela.example,50\nVela Robotics,https://vela.example,\nQuarry,,\n', HTTP_IMPORT_LIMITS),
      { Name: 'company.name', Domain: 'company.domain', Size: 'company.size' },
      { ...setup, target: 'companies' },
    );
    expect(rows.map((row) => [row.company?.name, row.company?.domains, row.duplicateOf])).toEqual([
      ['Vela', ['vela.example'], null],
      ['Vela Robotics', ['vela.example'], 1],
      ['Quarry', [], null],
    ]);
  });
});
```

```ts
export const IMPORT_FORMATS = ['csv', 'json'] as const;
export type ImportFormat = (typeof IMPORT_FORMATS)[number];

export const IMPORT_TARGETS = ['people', 'companies', 'leads'] as const;
export type ImportTarget = (typeof IMPORT_TARGETS)[number];

export const IMPORT_TARGET_LABELS: Readonly<Record<ImportTarget, string>> = {
  people: 'People',
  companies: 'Companies',
  leads: 'Leads',
};

export const IMPORT_DEFAULT_OWNERS = ['me', 'none'] as const;
export type ImportDefaultOwner = (typeof IMPORT_DEFAULT_OWNERS)[number];

export const MAX_IMPORT_BYTES = 2_000_000;
export const MAX_IMPORT_ROWS = 2_000;
export const MAX_CLI_IMPORT_ROWS = 50_000;
export const MAX_IMPORT_COLUMNS = 100;
export const MAX_IMPORT_CELL_LENGTH = 5_000;
export const IMPORT_CHUNK_ROWS = 100;
export const IMPORT_PREVIEW_CHUNK_ROWS = 500;

export interface ImportLimits {
  readonly maxBytes: number;
  readonly maxRows: number;
}

export const HTTP_IMPORT_LIMITS: ImportLimits = {
  maxBytes: MAX_IMPORT_BYTES,
  maxRows: MAX_IMPORT_ROWS,
};

export const CLI_IMPORT_LIMITS: ImportLimits = {
  maxBytes: 50_000_000,
  maxRows: MAX_CLI_IMPORT_ROWS,
};
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write `table.ts`**

`packages/shared/src/import/table.ts`:

```ts
import { payloadTooLarge, unsupportedMediaType, validationFailed } from '../errors/index.ts';
import {
  type ImportFormat,
  type ImportLimits,
  MAX_IMPORT_CELL_LENGTH,
  MAX_IMPORT_COLUMNS,
} from './constants.ts';

export interface ImportTable {
  readonly headers: readonly string[];
  readonly rows: readonly (readonly string[])[];
}

const DELIMITERS = [',', ';', '\t'] as const;
const BYTE_ORDER_MARK = '﻿';

export function utf8Length(text: string): number {
  return new TextEncoder().encode(text).byteLength;
}

function megabytes(bytes: number): string {
  return `${(bytes / 1_000_000).toFixed(1)} MB`;
}

function assertImportSize(content: string, limits: ImportLimits): void {
  const bytes = utf8Length(content);
  if (bytes > limits.maxBytes) {
    throw payloadTooLarge(
      `This file is ${megabytes(bytes)}. Import files up to ${megabytes(limits.maxBytes)}, or split it.`,
    );
  }
  if (content.includes('\u0000')) {
    throw unsupportedMediaType('This does not look like a text file. Export it as CSV or JSON and try again.');
  }
}

function withoutMark(content: string): string {
  return content.startsWith(BYTE_ORDER_MARK) ? content.slice(1) : content;
}

function detectDelimiter(text: string): string {
  const firstLine = text.split(/\r?\n/, 1)[0] ?? '';
  let best: string = ',';
  let bestCount = 0;
  for (const delimiter of DELIMITERS) {
    let count = 0;
    let quoted = false;
    for (const char of firstLine) {
      if (char === '"') quoted = !quoted;
      else if (!quoted && char === delimiter) count += 1;
    }
    if (count > bestCount) {
      best = delimiter;
      bestCount = count;
    }
  }
  return best;
}

function splitRecords(text: string, delimiter: string): string[][] {
  const records: string[][] = [];
  let record: string[] = [];
  let cell = '';
  let quoted = false;
  let quoteLine = 0;
  let line = 1;
  for (let index = 0; index < text.length; index += 1) {
    const char = text[index] ?? '';
    if (quoted) {
      if (char === '"') {
        if (text[index + 1] === '"') {
          cell += '"';
          index += 1;
        } else {
          quoted = false;
        }
      } else {
        if (char === '\n') line += 1;
        cell += char;
      }
      continue;
    }
    if (char === '"' && cell.length === 0) {
      quoted = true;
      quoteLine = line;
      continue;
    }
    if (char === delimiter) {
      record.push(cell);
      cell = '';
      continue;
    }
    if (char === '\r' || char === '\n') {
      if (char === '\r' && text[index + 1] === '\n') index += 1;
      record.push(cell);
      records.push(record);
      record = [];
      cell = '';
      line += 1;
      continue;
    }
    cell += char;
  }
  if (quoted) {
    throw validationFailed(`The file ends inside a quoted value that starts on line ${quoteLine}.`);
  }
  if (cell.length > 0 || record.length > 0) {
    record.push(cell);
    records.push(record);
  }
  return records;
}

function uniqueHeaders(raw: readonly string[]): string[] {
  const seen = new Map<string, number>();
  return raw.map((header, index) => {
    const base = header.trim() === '' ? `Column ${index + 1}` : header.trim();
    const count = (seen.get(base) ?? 0) + 1;
    seen.set(base, count);
    return count === 1 ? base : `${base} (${count})`;
  });
}

function tableOf(
  rawHeaders: readonly string[],
  rows: readonly (readonly string[])[],
  limits: ImportLimits,
): ImportTable {
  if (rawHeaders.length > MAX_IMPORT_COLUMNS) {
    throw validationFailed(
      `This file has ${rawHeaders.length} columns. Import at most ${MAX_IMPORT_COLUMNS}.`,
    );
  }
  if (rows.length > limits.maxRows) {
    throw payloadTooLarge(
      `This file has ${rows.length} rows. Import at most ${limits.maxRows} at a time, or split the file.`,
    );
  }
  if (rows.length === 0) throw validationFailed('This file has a header but no rows.');
  const headers = uniqueHeaders(rawHeaders);
  rows.forEach((row, index) => {
    row.forEach((cell, column) => {
      if (cell.length > MAX_IMPORT_CELL_LENGTH) {
        throw validationFailed(
          `Row ${index + 1}, column ${headers[column] ?? String(column + 1)} is longer than ${MAX_IMPORT_CELL_LENGTH} characters.`,
        );
      }
    });
  });
  return { headers, rows: rows.map((row) => row.map((cell) => cell.trim())) };
}

export function parseCsv(content: string, limits: ImportLimits): ImportTable {
  assertImportSize(content, limits);
  const text = withoutMark(content);
  const records = splitRecords(text, detectDelimiter(text)).filter((record) =>
    record.some((cell) => cell.trim().length > 0),
  );
  const [header, ...rows] = records;
  if (header === undefined) throw validationFailed('This file is empty.');
  return tableOf(header, rows, limits);
}

function cellOf(value: unknown, row: number, key: string): string {
  if (value === undefined || value === null) return '';
  if (typeof value === 'string') return value;
  if (typeof value === 'number' || typeof value === 'boolean') return String(value);
  if (Array.isArray(value) && value.every((item) => typeof item === 'string' || typeof item === 'number')) {
    return value.map(String).join('; ');
  }
  throw validationFailed(
    `Row ${row}, key ${key} holds a nested value. Flatten it to text, a number, true or false, or a list of text.`,
  );
}

function isRecord(value: unknown): value is Record<string, unknown> {
  return typeof value === 'object' && value !== null && !Array.isArray(value);
}

export function parseJsonRecords(content: string, limits: ImportLimits): ImportTable {
  assertImportSize(content, limits);
  let parsed: unknown;
  try {
    parsed = JSON.parse(withoutMark(content)) as unknown;
  } catch {
    throw validationFailed('This file is not valid JSON.');
  }
  if (!Array.isArray(parsed)) {
    throw validationFailed('A JSON import is an array of objects, one per row.');
  }
  const headers: string[] = [];
  const seen = new Set<string>();
  const objects = parsed.map((entry: unknown, index) => {
    if (!isRecord(entry)) throw validationFailed(`Row ${index + 1} is not an object.`);
    for (const key of Object.keys(entry)) {
      if (!seen.has(key)) {
        seen.add(key);
        headers.push(key);
      }
    }
    return entry;
  });
  const rows = objects.map((entry, index) => headers.map((key) => cellOf(entry[key], index + 1, key)));
  return tableOf(headers, rows, limits);
}

export function parseImportTable(
  format: ImportFormat,
  content: string,
  limits: ImportLimits,
): ImportTable {
  return format === 'json' ? parseJsonRecords(content, limits) : parseCsv(content, limits);
}
```

The source carries the escapes `﻿` and `\u0000` as six ASCII characters each, never the literal characters (the same rule M0.2 applied to its regexes).

```ts
  'person.lastName': 'Last name',
  'person.email': 'Email',

  'person.linkedinUrl': 'LinkedIn URL',
  'person.linkedinProviderId': 'LinkedIn id',
  'person.location': 'Location',
  'person.timezone': 'Time zone',

  'company.domain': 'Company domain',
  'company.size': 'Company size',
```
