```ts
import { assertWithinRequestRateLimit, commitImport } from '@gravity/core';
import { assertCan } from '@gravity/shared/policy';
import { apiContext, handleRoute, publish } from '@/lib/api/handler.ts';
import { IMPORT_COMMIT_RATE, readImportBody } from '@/lib/api/imports.ts';
import { originClientIdOf } from '@/lib/api/write.ts';

export const dynamic = 'force-dynamic';
export const maxDuration = 300;

export async function POST(request: Request): Promise<Response> {
  return await handleRoute(async () => {
    const context = await apiContext();
    assertCan(context.principal, 'import:run');
    const body = await readImportBody(request);
    await assertWithinRequestRateLimit(
      `import:commit:${context.principal.userId}`,
      IMPORT_COMMIT_RATE,
      'You have started many imports in the last hour.',
    );
    const report = await commitImport(
      { principal: context.principal, originClientId: originClientIdOf(request) },
      body,
      { actorName: context.userName, publish },
    );
    return { report };
  });
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write the CLI**

`packages/core/src/import/cli.ts`:

```ts
import { db, eq, schema } from '@gravity/db';
import {
  CLI_IMPORT_LIMITS,
  IMPORT_FORMATS,
  IMPORT_TARGETS,
  type ImportMapping,
  type ImportReport,
  importMappingSchema,
  parseImportTable,
  suggestMapping,
} from '@gravity/shared/import';
import type { Principal } from '@gravity/shared/policy';
import { emailSchema } from '@gravity/shared/validators';
import { z } from 'zod';
import { listFieldDefinitions } from '../crm/field-service.ts';
import { listPipelines } from '../crm/pipeline-service.ts';
import { resolvePrincipal } from '../org/member-service.ts';
import { getOrganizationBySlug } from '../org/organization-service.ts';
import { flushOutbox } from '../realtime/outbox.ts';
import { commitImport, previewImport } from './import-service.ts';

export interface CliIo {
  readonly readText: (path: string) => Promise<string>;
  readonly print: (line: string) => void;
}

const USAGE =
  'Usage: bun run import <csv|json> <file> --workspace <slug> --as <email> --target <people|companies|leads> [--pipeline <KEY>] [--mapping <file.json>] [--source <name>] [--commit]';

const argsSchema = z.object({
  adapter: z.enum(IMPORT_FORMATS),
  path: z.string().min(1),
  workspace: z.string().min(1),
  as: emailSchema,
  target: z.enum(IMPORT_TARGETS),
  pipeline: z.string().min(1).optional(),
  mapping: z.string().min(1).optional(),
  source: z.string().min(1).optional(),
  commit: z.boolean(),
});
export type CliArgs = z.infer<typeof argsSchema>;

const mappingFileSchema = z.object({ columns: importMappingSchema });

export function parseCliArgs(argv: readonly string[]): CliArgs {
  const positional: string[] = [];
  const flags = new Map<string, string>();
  let commit = false;
  for (let index = 0; index < argv.length; index += 1) {
    const token = argv[index] ?? '';
    if (!token.startsWith('--')) {
      positional.push(token);
      continue;
    }
    const name = token.slice(2);
    if (name === 'commit') {
      commit = true;
      continue;
    }
    const value = argv[index + 1];
    if (value === undefined || value.startsWith('--')) throw new Error(`--${name} needs a value. ${USAGE}`);
    flags.set(name, value);
    index += 1;
  }
  const parsed = argsSchema.safeParse({
    adapter: positional[0],
    path: positional[1],
    workspace: flags.get('workspace'),
    as: flags.get('as'),
    target: flags.get('target'),
    pipeline: flags.get('pipeline'),
    mapping: flags.get('mapping'),
    source: flags.get('source'),
    commit,
  });
  if (parsed.success) return parsed.data;
  const issue = parsed.error.issues[0];
  throw new Error(`${issue?.path.join('.') ?? 'arguments'}: ${issue?.message ?? 'invalid'}. ${USAGE}`);
}

async function pipelineIdOf(principal: Principal, args: CliArgs): Promise<string | null> {
  if (args.target !== 'leads') return null;
  const wanted = args.pipeline;
  if (wanted === undefined) throw new Error('A leads import needs --pipeline <KEY>.');
  const pipelines = await listPipelines(principal);
  const pipeline = pipelines.find((entry) => entry.key === wanted.toUpperCase() || entry.id === wanted);
  if (pipeline === undefined) {
    throw new Error(`There is no pipeline ${wanted}. Pipelines: ${pipelines.map((entry) => entry.key).join(', ')}.`);
  }
  return pipeline.id;
}

function mappingOf(text: string): ImportMapping {
  let raw: unknown;
  try {
    raw = JSON.parse(text) as unknown;
  } catch {
    throw new Error('The mapping file is not valid JSON.');
  }
  return mappingFileSchema.parse(raw).columns;
}

function summaryOf(report: ImportReport): string[] {
  const totals = report.totals;
  return [
    `${report.mode === 'preview' ? 'Dry run' : 'Imported'}: ${totals.rows} rows, ${totals.created} new, ${totals.merged} merged, ${totals.unchanged} unchanged, ${totals.skipped} skipped, ${totals.invalid} invalid, ${totals.companiesCreated} companies created, ${totals.leadsCreated} leads created, ${totals.leadsExisting} leads already open.`,
    ...report.rows.flatMap((row) =>
      row.issues.map(
        (issue) => `Row ${issue.row}${issue.column === null ? '' : ` (${issue.column})`}: ${issue.message}`,
      ),
    ),
    ...(report.failure === null ? [] : [`Stopped at row ${report.failure.row}: ${report.failure.message}`]),
  ];
}

export async function runImportCli(argv: readonly string[], io: CliIo): Promise<number> {
  try {
    const args = parseCliArgs(argv);
    const organization = await getOrganizationBySlug(args.workspace);
    const [user] = await db.select().from(schema.user).where(eq(schema.user.email, args.as)).limit(1);
    if (user === undefined) throw new Error(`No user has the email ${args.as}.`);
    const principal = await resolvePrincipal(user.id, organization.id);
    const content = await io.readText(args.path);
    const pipelineId = await pipelineIdOf(principal, args);
    const mapping =
      args.mapping === undefined
        ? suggestMapping(
            parseImportTable(args.adapter, content, CLI_IMPORT_LIMITS).headers,
            args.target,
            await listFieldDefinitions(principal),
          )
        : mappingOf(await io.readText(args.mapping));
    const request = {
      format: args.adapter,
      content,
      target: args.target,
      pipelineId,
      mapping,
      source: args.source ?? 'import',
      defaultOwner: 'me',
    };
    const report = args.commit
      ? await commitImport({ principal }, request, {
          actorName: user.name,
          limits: CLI_IMPORT_LIMITS,
          publish: async (actions) => {
            await flushOutbox(actions.map((action) => action.syncId));
          },
        })
      : await previewImport(principal, request, { limits: CLI_IMPORT_LIMITS });
    for (const line of summaryOf(report)) io.print(line);
    if (!args.commit) io.print('Nothing was written. Run again with --commit to import.');
    return report.status === 'completed' ? 0 : 1;
  } catch (error: unknown) {
    io.print(error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error));
    return 1;
  }
}
```

`packages/core/src/import/run-cli.ts`:

```ts
import { readFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
import { closeRealtime } from '../realtime/publisher.ts';
import { runImportCli } from './cli.ts';

const code = await runImportCli(process.argv.slice(2), {
  readText: (path) => readFile(path, 'utf8'),
  print: (line) => console.info(line),
});
await closeRealtime();
process.exit(code);
```

In the root `package.json` add the script `"import": "bun --env-file=.env packages/core/src/import/run-cli.ts"`. In `README.md` under Develop add: `bun run import csv people.csv --workspace <slug> --as <email> --target people` dry runs an import; add `--commit` to write it.

---
### Task 15: Import page

An `/import` page in the app shell, driven from the keyboard: `O` chooses a file, the mapping is suggested from the headers and editable per column, `Cmd+Enter` previews and then imports, `Esc` steps back. It shows the dry run before anything is written (counts, then a 28px-row table of outcomes with each issue and what a person's edits kept), then the result with a link into the list. Empty states point at it, and the palette has an Import command. Wide screens only, as the UI spec says for bulk actions.

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/web/src/app/(app)/import/page.tsx`
- Create: `apps/web/src/features/import/import-screen.tsx`, `apps/web/src/features/import/import-view.tsx`, `apps/web/src/features/import/mapping-table.tsx`, `apps/web/src/features/import/import-report.tsx`, `apps/web/src/features/import/use-import.ts`
- Modify: `apps/web/src/components/command-palette.tsx`, `apps/web/src/features/leads/leads-view.tsx`
- Test: `apps/web/tests/features/import/import-view.test.tsx`, `apps/web/tests/components/command-palette.test.tsx` (append)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `parseImportTable`, `suggestMapping`, `mappingIssues`, `columnAllowed`, `customColumn`, `importColumnLabel`, `IMPORT_STANDARD_COLUMNS`, `IMPORT_TARGETS`, `IMPORT_TARGET_LABELS`, `HTTP_IMPORT_LIMITS`, `MAX_IMPORT_BYTES`, `importReportSchema`, `ImportReport`, `ImportTable`, `ImportMapping`, `ImportColumn`, `ImportTarget`, `ImportFormat` (Task 11); `POST /api/imports/preview`, `POST /api/imports` (Task 14); `useWorkspace`, `useCan`, `SettingsGate`, `useHotkey`, `useToast`, `apiFetch`, `messageOf`, UI kit.
- Produces: `ImportView({ initialTarget, initialPipelineKey })`, `ImportScreen` (same props), `MappingTable`, `ImportReportView({ report })`, `useImportPreview()`, `useImportCommit()`, `interface ImportDraft`; palette command id `import`.

`apps/web/src/features/import/use-import.ts`:

```ts
'use client';

import {
  type ImportDefaultOwner,
  type ImportFormat,
  type ImportMapping,
  type ImportReport,
  type ImportTarget,
  importReportSchema,
} from '@gravity/shared/import';
import { useMutation } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { z } from 'zod';
import { apiFetch } from '@/lib/api/client.ts';

export interface ImportDraft {
  readonly format: ImportFormat;
  readonly content: string;
  readonly target: ImportTarget;
  readonly pipelineId: string | null;
  readonly mapping: ImportMapping;
  readonly source: string;
  readonly defaultOwner: ImportDefaultOwner;
}

const responseSchema = z.object({ report: importReportSchema });

async function send(path: string, draft: ImportDraft): Promise<ImportReport> {
  return (await apiFetch(path, responseSchema, { method: 'POST', body: draft })).report;
}

export function useImportPreview() {
  return useMutation({ mutationFn: (draft: ImportDraft) => send('/api/imports/preview', draft) });
}

export function useImportCommit() {
  return useMutation({ mutationFn: (draft: ImportDraft) => send('/api/imports', draft) });
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write the mapping table and the report**

`apps/web/src/features/import/mapping-table.tsx`:

```tsx
'use client';

import {
  columnAllowed,
  customColumn,
  IMPORT_STANDARD_COLUMNS,
  type ImportColumn,
  type ImportMapping,
  type ImportTable,
  type ImportTarget,
  importColumnLabel,
} from '@gravity/shared/import';
import type { FieldDefinitionRow } from '@gravity/shared/records';
import { NativeSelect } from '@/components/ui/native-select.tsx';

interface ColumnOption {
  readonly value: ImportColumn;
  readonly label: string;
  readonly group: string;
}

const GROUP_ORDER = ['Skip', 'Identity', 'Person', 'Company', 'Lead', 'Custom fields'] as const;
const SAMPLE_LENGTH = 60;

function groupOf(column: ImportColumn): string {
  if (column === 'ignore') return 'Skip';
  if (column === 'sourceId') return 'Identity';
  if (column.startsWith('person.')) return 'Person';
  if (column.startsWith('company.')) return 'Company';
  return 'Lead';
}

export function columnOptions(target: ImportTarget, fields: readonly FieldDefinitionRow[]): ColumnOption[] {
  const standard = IMPORT_STANDARD_COLUMNS.filter((column) => columnAllowed(target, column)).map((column) => ({
    value: column,
    label: importColumnLabel(column),
    group: groupOf(column),
  }));
  const custom = fields.flatMap((field) =>
    field.object === 'deal'
      ? []
      : [{ value: customColumn(field.object, field.key), label: field.label, group: 'Custom fields' }],
  );
  return [...standard, ...custom.filter((option) => columnAllowed(target, option.value))];
}

function sampleOf(table: ImportTable, header: string): string {
  const index = table.headers.indexOf(header);
  const sample = table.rows.slice(0, 3).map((row) => row[index] ?? '').find((cell) => cell.length > 0) ?? '';
  return sample.length > SAMPLE_LENGTH ? `${sample.slice(0, SAMPLE_LENGTH)}...` : sample;
}

export interface MappingTableProps {
  readonly table: ImportTable;
  readonly target: ImportTarget;
  readonly mapping: ImportMapping;
  readonly fields: readonly FieldDefinitionRow[];
  readonly onChange: (header: string, column: ImportColumn) => void;
}

export function MappingTable({ table, target, mapping, fields, onChange }: MappingTableProps) {
  const options = columnOptions(target, fields);
  return (
    <table className="w-full table-fixed text-dense">
      <caption className="sr-only">Map each column of the file to a Gravity field</caption>
      <thead>
        <tr className="h-7 text-left text-2xs text-faint uppercase tracking-wide">
          <th className="w-1/3 font-medium">Column</th>
          <th className="w-1/3 font-medium">Sample</th>
          <th className="w-1/3 font-medium">Gravity field</th>
        </tr>
      </thead>
      <tbody>
        {table.headers.map((header) => (
          <tr key={header} className="h-7 border-border border-t">
            <td className="truncate pr-3 text-text">{header}</td>
            <td className="truncate pr-3 text-muted">{sampleOf(table, header)}</td>
            <td>
              <NativeSelect
                aria-label={`Field for ${header}`}
                className="h-7 w-full text-xs"
                value={mapping[header] ?? 'ignore'}
                onChange={(event) => {
                  const next = options.find((option) => option.value === event.target.value);
                  if (next !== undefined) onChange(header, next.value);
                }}
              >
                {GROUP_ORDER.map((group) => {
                  const members = options.filter((option) => option.group === group);
                  return members.length === 0 ? null : (
                    <optgroup key={group} label={group}>
                      {members.map((option) => (
                        <option key={option.value} value={option.value}>
                          {option.label}
                        </option>
                      ))}
                    </optgroup>
                  );
                })}
              </NativeSelect>
            </td>
          </tr>
        ))}
      </tbody>
    </table>
  );
}
```

`apps/web/src/features/import/import-report.tsx`:

```tsx
'use client';

import type { ImportReport, ImportRowOutcome } from '@gravity/shared/import';
import { Badge } from '@/components/ui/badge.tsx';

const SHOWN_ROWS = 200;

const STATUS_LABELS: Readonly<Record<ImportRowOutcome['status'], string>> = {
  create: 'New',
  merge: 'Merged',
  unchanged: 'Unchanged',
  skipped: 'Skipped',
  invalid: 'Invalid',
};

const STATUS_TONES = {
  create: 'accent',
  merge: 'neutral',
  unchanged: 'outline',
  skipped: 'warning',
  invalid: 'danger',
} as const;

function leadText(row: ImportRowOutcome, preview: boolean): string {
  if (row.lead === 'exists') return `${row.leadKey ?? 'Open lead'} already open`;
  if (row.lead === 'create') return preview ? 'New lead' : (row.leadKey ?? 'New lead');
  return '';
}

function noteText(row: ImportRowOutcome): string {
  if (row.issues.length > 0) return row.issues.map((issue) => issue.message).join(' ');
  if (row.kept.length > 0) return `Kept what a person set: ${row.kept.join(', ')}`;
  if (row.changes.length > 0) return `Updates ${row.changes.join(', ')}`;
  return '';
}

export function ImportReportView({ report }: { readonly report: ImportReport }) {
  const preview = report.mode === 'preview';
  const totals = report.totals;
  const chips: readonly (readonly [number, string])[] = [
    [totals.created, 'new'],
    [totals.merged, 'merged'],
    [totals.unchanged, 'unchanged'],
    [totals.skipped, 'skipped'],
    [totals.invalid, 'invalid'],
    [totals.companiesCreated, preview ? 'companies to create' : 'companies created'],
    [totals.leadsCreated, preview ? 'leads to create' : 'leads created'],
    [totals.leadsExisting, 'leads already open'],
  ];
  return (
    <section aria-label={preview ? 'Preview' : 'Result'} className="flex flex-col gap-3">
      <ul aria-label="Totals" className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
        {chips
          .filter(([total, label]) => total > 0 || label === 'new')
          .map(([total, label]) => (
            <li key={label}>
              <Badge data-numeric>{`${total} ${label}`}</Badge>
            </li>
          ))}
      </ul>
      {report.failure === null ? null : (
        <p role="alert" className="text-danger text-dense">
          Stopped at row {report.failure.row}: {report.failure.message}
        </p>
      )}
      <table className="w-full table-fixed text-dense">
        <caption className="sr-only">{preview ? 'What the import will do' : 'What the import did'}</caption>
        <thead>
          <tr className="h-7 text-left text-2xs text-faint uppercase tracking-wide">
            <th className="w-12 font-medium">Row</th>
            <th className="w-48 font-medium">Record</th>
            <th className="w-24 font-medium">Status</th>
            <th className="w-36 font-medium">Lead</th>
            <th className="font-medium">Notes</th>
          </tr>
        </thead>
        <tbody>
          {report.rows.slice(0, SHOWN_ROWS).map((row) => (
            <tr key={row.row} className="h-7 border-border border-t">
              <td data-numeric className="text-faint">
                {row.row}
              </td>
              <td className="truncate pr-3 text-text">{row.label}</td>
              <td>
                <Badge tone={STATUS_TONES[row.status]}>{STATUS_LABELS[row.status]}</Badge>
              </td>
              <td className="truncate pr-3 text-muted">{leadText(row, preview)}</td>
              <td className="truncate text-muted" title={noteText(row)}>
                {noteText(row)}
              </td>
            </tr>
          ))}
        </tbody>
      </table>
      {report.rows.length > SHOWN_ROWS ? (
        <p className="text-2xs text-faint">
          Showing the first {SHOWN_ROWS} of {report.rows.length} rows.
        </p>
      ) : null}
    </section>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Write the view, the screen and the page**

`apps/web/src/features/import/import-view.tsx`:

```tsx
'use client';

import {
  HTTP_IMPORT_LIMITS,
  IMPORT_TARGET_LABELS,
  IMPORT_TARGETS,
  type ImportColumn,
  type ImportFormat,
  type ImportMapping,
  type ImportReport,
  type ImportTable,
  type ImportTarget,
  MAX_IMPORT_BYTES,
  mappingIssues,
  parseImportTable,
  suggestMapping,
} from '@gravity/shared/import';
import type { FieldDefinitionRow } from '@gravity/shared/records';
import { FileUp } from 'lucide-react';
import Link from 'next/link';
import { useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button.tsx';
import { EmptyState } from '@/components/ui/empty-state.tsx';
import { Input } from '@/components/ui/input.tsx';
import { Kbd } from '@/components/ui/kbd.tsx';
import { NativeSelect } from '@/components/ui/native-select.tsx';
import { useToast } from '@/components/ui/toast.tsx';
import { useCan } from '@/features/workspace/use-can.ts';
import { useWorkspace } from '@/features/workspace/use-workspace.ts';
import { messageOf } from '@/lib/api/client.ts';
import { useHotkey } from '@/lib/keyboard/index.ts';
import { ImportReportView } from './import-report.tsx';
import { MappingTable } from './mapping-table.tsx';
import { type ImportDraft, useImportCommit, useImportPreview } from './use-import.ts';

interface LoadedFile {
  readonly name: string;
  readonly format: ImportFormat;
  readonly content: string;
  readonly table: ImportTable;
}

export interface ImportViewProps {
  readonly initialTarget: ImportTarget;
  readonly initialPipelineKey: string | null;
}

function targetOf(value: string): ImportTarget {
  return IMPORT_TARGETS.find((target) => target === value) ?? 'people';
}

export function ImportView({ initialTarget, initialPipelineKey }: ImportViewProps) {
  const workspace = useWorkspace();
  const canImport = useCan('import:run');
  const { toast } = useToast();
  const input = useRef<HTMLInputElement | null>(null);
  const [target, setTarget] = useState<ImportTarget>(initialTarget);
  const [pipelineId, setPipelineId] = useState<string | null>(
    workspace.pipelineByKey.get(initialPipelineKey ?? '')?.id ?? workspace.pipelines[0]?.id ?? null,
  );
  const [source, setSource] = useState('import');
  const [file, setFile] = useState<LoadedFile | null>(null);
  const [mapping, setMapping] = useState<ImportMapping>({});
  const [report, setReport] = useState<ImportReport | null>(null);
  const [result, setResult] = useState<ImportReport | null>(null);
  const [failure, setFailure] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const preview = useImportPreview();
  const commit = useImportCommit();
  const pipeline = pipelineId === null ? undefined : workspace.pipelineById.get(pipelineId);

  function definitionsFor(nextTarget: ImportTarget, nextPipelineId: string | null): FieldDefinitionRow[] {
    return workspace.allFields.filter(
      (field) =>
        field.object !== 'lead' ||
        (nextTarget === 'leads' && (field.pipelineId === null || field.pipelineId === nextPipelineId)),
    );
  }

  function remap(table: ImportTable, nextTarget: ImportTarget, nextPipelineId: string | null): void {
    setMapping(suggestMapping(table.headers, nextTarget, definitionsFor(nextTarget, nextPipelineId)));
    setReport(null);
  }

  async function load(chosen: File): Promise<void> {
    setFailure(null);
    setResult(null);
    setReport(null);
    if (chosen.size > MAX_IMPORT_BYTES) {
      setFailure(`${chosen.name} is larger than 2.0 MB. Split it, or use bun run import.`);
      return;
    }
    const format: ImportFormat = chosen.name.toLowerCase().endsWith('.json') ? 'json' : 'csv';
    const content = await chosen.text();
    try {
      const table = parseImportTable(format, content, HTTP_IMPORT_LIMITS);
      setFile({ name: chosen.name, format, content, table });
      remap(table, target, pipelineId);
    } catch (error: unknown) {
      setFile(null);
      setFailure(messageOf(error, 'This file could not be read.'));
    }
  }

  const issues =
    file === null
      ? []
      : [
          ...(target === 'leads' && pipelineId === null ? ['Choose the pipeline the leads go into.'] : []),
          ...mappingIssues(mapping, target, file.table.headers, {
            person: workspace.fieldsFor('person', null).map((field) => field.key),
            company: workspace.fieldsFor('company', null).map((field) => field.key),
            lead: pipelineId === null ? [] : workspace.fieldsFor('lead', pipelineId).map((field) => field.key),
          }),
        ];

  function draftOf(loaded: LoadedFile): ImportDraft {
    return {
      format: loaded.format,
      content: loaded.content,
      target,
      pipelineId: target === 'leads' ? pipelineId : null,
      mapping,
      source,
      defaultOwner: 'me',
    };
  }

  function runPreview(): void {
    if (file === null || preview.isPending) return;
    const first = issues[0];
    if (first !== undefined) {
      setFailure(first);
      return;
    }
    setFailure(null);
    preview.mutate(draftOf(file), {
      onSuccess: setReport,
      onError: (error) => setFailure(messageOf(error, 'The preview did not complete. Try again.')),
    });
  }

  function runCommit(): void {
    if (file === null || commit.isPending) return;
    setFailure(null);
    commit.mutate(draftOf(file), {
      onSuccess: (done) => {
        setResult(done);
        setReport(null);
        toast(
          done.status === 'completed'
            ? { title: `Imported ${done.totals.processed} rows`, tone: 'success' }
            : { title: 'The import stopped part way', description: done.failure?.message ?? '', tone: 'danger' },
        );
      },
      onError: (error) => setFailure(messageOf(error, 'The import did not complete. Try again.')),
    });
  }

  function next(): void {
    if (!canImport || result !== null) return;
    if (report !== null) runCommit();
    else runPreview();
  }

  function back(): void {
    if (report !== null) setReport(null);
    else if (file !== null && result === null) setFile(null);
  }

  function reset(): void {
    setFile(null);
    setReport(null);
    setResult(null);
    setFailure(null);
  }

  useHotkey('mod+enter', next, { label: 'Preview, then run the import', allowInInput: true });
  useHotkey('escape', back, { label: 'Go back a step', enabled: report !== null || file !== null });
  useHotkey('o', () => input.current?.click(), { label: 'Choose a file', enabled: file === null && canImport });

  const listHref =
    target === 'leads' && pipeline !== undefined ? `/leads/${pipeline.key}` : target === 'companies' ? '/companies' : '/people';
  const listLabel =
    target === 'leads' && pipeline !== undefined ? `Open the ${pipeline.key} leads` : `Open ${IMPORT_TARGET_LABELS[target]}`;

  return (
    <div className="mx-auto flex w-full max-w-4xl flex-col gap-6 px-6 py-6">
      <header className="flex flex-col gap-1">
        <h1 className="font-medium text-lg text-text">Import</h1>
        <p className="text-dense text-muted">
          Bring people, companies and leads in from a CSV or JSON file. Nothing is written until you
          have seen the preview.
        </p>
      </header>
      {canImport ? null : (
        <p role="alert" className="text-dense text-danger">
          Your role cannot run imports. Ask a member or an admin of this workspace.
        </p>
      )}
      <section aria-label="Source" className="flex flex-wrap items-end gap-3">
        <label className="flex flex-col gap-1 text-2xs text-faint">
          What to import
          <NativeSelect
            value={target}
            onChange={(event) => {
              const nextTarget = targetOf(event.target.value);
              setTarget(nextTarget);
              if (file !== null) remap(file.table, nextTarget, pipelineId);
            }}
          >
            {IMPORT_TARGETS.map((option) => (
              <option key={option} value={option}>
                {IMPORT_TARGET_LABELS[option]}
              </option>
            ))}
          </NativeSelect>
        </label>
        {target === 'leads' ? (
          <label className="flex flex-col gap-1 text-2xs text-faint">
            Pipeline
            <NativeSelect
              value={pipelineId ?? ''}
              onChange={(event) => {
                const nextPipeline = event.target.value === '' ? null : event.target.value;
                setPipelineId(nextPipeline);
                if (file !== null) remap(file.table, target, nextPipeline);
              }}
            >
              {workspace.pipelines.map((option) => (
                <option key={option.id} value={option.id}>
                  {`${workspace.brandById.get(option.brandId)?.name ?? ''} · ${option.name} (${option.key})`}
                </option>
              ))}
            </NativeSelect>
          </label>
        ) : null}
        <label className="flex flex-col gap-1 text-2xs text-faint">
          Source name
          <Input value={source} onChange={(event) => setSource(event.target.value)} className="w-40" />
        </label>
        <Button onClick={() => input.current?.click()} disabled={!canImport}>
          {file === null ? 'Choose a file' : `Replace ${file.name}`}
          <Kbd keys={['o']} aria-hidden="true" />
        </Button>
        <input
          ref={input}
          type="file"
          accept=".csv,.json,text/csv,application/json"
          aria-label="Import file"
          className="sr-only"
          onChange={(event) => {
            const chosen = event.target.files?.[0];
            event.target.value = '';
            if (chosen !== undefined) load(chosen).catch(() => undefined);
          }}
        />
      </section>
      {failure === null ? null : (
        <p role="alert" className="text-dense text-danger">
          {failure}
        </p>
      )}
      {file === null && result === null ? (
        <EmptyState
          icon={<FileUp />}
          title="Choose a CSV or JSON file."
          description="Up to 2,000 rows and 2 MB. People match by source id, email and LinkedIn URL, and companies by domain, so running the same file again adds nothing new."
        />
      ) : null}
      {file !== null && report === null && result === null ? (
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-3">
          <MappingTable
            table={file.table}
            target={target}
            mapping={mapping}
            fields={definitionsFor(target, pipelineId)}
            onChange={(header: string, column: ImportColumn) => {
              setMapping({ ...mapping, [header]: column });
              setReport(null);
            }}
          />
          <div className="flex justify-end">
            <Button variant="primary" onClick={runPreview}>
              Preview {file.table.rows.length} rows
              <Kbd keys={['mod', 'enter']} aria-hidden="true" />
            </Button>
          </div>
        </div>
      ) : null}
      {report !== null ? (
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-3">
          <ImportReportView report={report} />
          <div className="flex justify-end gap-2">
            <Button variant="ghost" onClick={back}>
              Back to the mapping
              <Kbd keys={['esc']} aria-hidden="true" />
            </Button>
            <Button variant="primary" onClick={runCommit}>
              Import {report.totals.rows - report.totals.invalid - report.totals.skipped} rows
              <Kbd keys={['mod', 'enter']} aria-hidden="true" />
            </Button>
          </div>
        </div>
      ) : null}
      {result !== null ? (
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-3">
          <ImportReportView report={result} />
          <div className="flex justify-end gap-2">
            <Button variant="ghost" onClick={reset}>
              Import another file
            </Button>
            <Button asChild variant="primary">
              <Link href={listHref}>{listLabel}</Link>
            </Button>
          </div>
        </div>
      ) : null}
    </div>
  );
}
```

`Kbd` shows `esc` through its glyph table; if `keyGlyph('esc')` renders `ESC`, that is acceptable copy.

```tsx
import { IMPORT_TARGETS } from '@gravity/shared/import';
import type { Metadata } from 'next';
import { ImportScreen } from '@/features/import/import-screen.tsx';

export const metadata: Metadata = { title: 'Import' };

export default async function ImportPage({
  searchParams,
}: {
  readonly searchParams: Promise<Record<string, string | string[] | undefined>>;
}) {
  const params = await searchParams;
  const rawTarget = typeof params['target'] === 'string' ? params['target'] : '';
  const pipeline = typeof params['pipeline'] === 'string' ? params['pipeline'] : null;
  return (
    <ImportScreen
      initialTarget={IMPORT_TARGETS.find((target) => target === rawTarget) ?? 'people'}
      initialPipelineKey={pipeline}
    />
  );
}
```

### Task 16: Generic demo seed

`bun run db:seed` builds a demo workspace that shows what Gravity is for, with fictional companies only (spec 12): two members, four brands for four kinds of outbound motion (a SaaS product, services, a marketplace recruiting sellers, and a fundraise), each with a prospecting pipeline, a person custom field, and leads spread across stages, owners and priorities. Two people are pursued by two brands, so one person carries two leads. The seed loads its leads through `commitImport`, so it exercises the same path as a user's first CSV.

**Files:**
- Create: `packages/core/src/seed/demo-seed.ts`, `packages/core/src/seed/run-seed.ts`
- Modify: `packages/core/src/index.ts`, `package.json` (script `db:seed`), `README.md` (one line under Develop)
- Test: `packages/core/tests/seed/demo-seed.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `createOrganization`, `resolvePrincipal`, `createBrand`, `createFieldDefinition` (M0.1 and M0.2); `commitImport` (Task 13); `parseDomainList` (`@gravity/shared/utils`); `BrandColor` (`@gravity/shared/constants`).
- Produces: `interface DemoSeedOptions { slug: string; domain: string }`, `DEMO_SEED_DEFAULTS = { slug: 'demo', domain: 'gravity.test' }`, `interface DemoBrand { name; key; domain; color: BrandColor; rows: readonly string[] }`, `DEMO_BRANDS: readonly DemoBrand[]`, `interface DemoSeedResult { organizationId; slug; ownerEmail; teammateEmail; brands: number; people: number; leads: number }`, `seedDemoWorkspace(options?: DemoSeedOptions): Promise<DemoSeedResult>`; root script `bun run db:seed [--slug <slug>] [--domain <domain>]`.

```ts
import { afterAll, beforeEach, describe, expect, test } from 'bun:test';
import { db, eq, schema } from '@gravity/db';
import { closeRealtime } from '../../src/realtime/publisher.ts';
import { DEMO_BRANDS, seedDemoWorkspace } from '../../src/seed/demo-seed.ts';
import { resetDatabase } from '../../src/test-support.ts';

beforeEach(async () => {
  await resetDatabase();
});

afterAll(async () => {
  await closeRealtime();
});

describe('seedDemoWorkspace', () => {
  test('builds four brands with leads across stages and owners, then refuses a second run', async () => {
    const result = await seedDemoWorkspace({ slug: 'demo-test', domain: 'gravity.test' });
    const expectedLeads = DEMO_BRANDS.reduce((total, brand) => total + brand.rows.length, 0);
    expect(result).toMatchObject({
      slug: 'demo-test',
      brands: 4,
      leads: expectedLeads,
      ownerEmail: 'alex@gravity.test',
      teammateEmail: 'sam@gravity.test',
    });
    expect(result.people).toBe(expectedLeads - 2);
    const pipelines = await db
      .select()
      .from(schema.pipeline)
      .where(eq(schema.pipeline.organizationId, result.organizationId));
    expect(pipelines.map((pipeline) => pipeline.key).sort()).toEqual(['ATL', 'HAR', 'LUM', 'SED']);
    const leads = await db.select().from(schema.lead).where(eq(schema.lead.organizationId, result.organizationId));
    expect(new Set(leads.map((lead) => lead.stageId)).size).toBeGreaterThan(5);
    expect(new Set(leads.map((lead) => lead.ownerId)).size).toBe(2);
    await expect(seedDemoWorkspace({ slug: 'demo-test', domain: 'gravity.test' })).rejects.toMatchObject({
      code: 'conflict',
    });
  });

  test('uses fictional companies on reserved domains only', async () => {
    const result = await seedDemoWorkspace({ slug: 'demo-domains', domain: 'gravity.test' });
    const companies = await db
      .select()
      .from(schema.company)
      .where(eq(schema.company.organizationId, result.organizationId));
    expect(companies.length).toBeGreaterThan(10);
    expect(companies.every((company) => company.domains.every((domain) => domain.endsWith('.example')))).toBe(true);
    const brands = await db.select().from(schema.brand).where(eq(schema.brand.organizationId, result.organizationId));
    expect(brands.every((brand) => brand.domain?.endsWith('.example') ?? false)).toBe(true);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the seed**

`packages/core/src/seed/demo-seed.ts`:

```ts
import { db, eq, schema } from '@gravity/db';
import type { BrandColor } from '@gravity/shared/constants';
import { conflict } from '@gravity/shared/errors';
import { createBrand } from '../crm/brand-service.ts';
import { createFieldDefinition } from '../crm/field-service.ts';
import { commitImport } from '../import/import-service.ts';
import { newId } from '../internal.ts';
import { resolvePrincipal } from '../org/member-service.ts';
import { createOrganization } from '../org/organization-service.ts';

export interface DemoSeedOptions {
  readonly slug: string;
  readonly domain: string;
}

export const DEMO_SEED_DEFAULTS: DemoSeedOptions = { slug: 'demo', domain: 'gravity.test' };

export interface DemoBrand {
  readonly name: string;
  readonly key: string;
  readonly domain: string;
  readonly color: BrandColor;
  readonly rows: readonly string[];
}

export interface DemoSeedResult {
  readonly organizationId: string;
  readonly slug: string;
  readonly ownerEmail: string;
  readonly teammateEmail: string;
  readonly brands: number;
  readonly people: number;
  readonly leads: number;
}

const HEADER = 'Name,Email,Title,Company,Domain,Stage,Owner,Priority,Seniority';

const SEED_MAPPING = {
  Name: 'person.name',
  Email: 'person.email',
  Title: 'person.title',
  Company: 'company.name',
  Domain: 'company.domain',
  Stage: 'lead.stage',
  Owner: 'lead.owner',
  Priority: 'lead.priority',
  Seniority: 'person.field.seniority',
} as const;

export const DEMO_BRANDS: readonly DemoBrand[] = [
  {
    name: 'Lumen Analytics',
    key: 'LUM',
    domain: 'lumen.example',
    color: 'blue',
    rows: [
      'Amara Okafor,amara@vela.example,VP Engineering,Vela Robotics,vela.example,New,OWNER,High,executive',
      'Liam Chen,liam@quarry.example,Head of Data,Quarry Labs,quarry.example,Researching,OWNER,Medium,senior',
      'Sofia Marquez,sofia@kestrel.example,CTO,Kestrel Analytics,kestrel.example,Contacted,TEAMMATE,Urgent,executive',
      'Ravi Patel,ravi@bluefin.example,Data Engineer,Bluefin Health,bluefin.example,Follow-up,TEAMMATE,Low,junior',
      'Hana Kim,hana@juniper.example,Analytics Lead,Juniper Bank,juniper.example,Replied,OWNER,High,senior',
      'Jonas Weber,jonas@ember.example,Engineering Manager,Ember Games,ember.example,Meeting booked,OWNER,Medium,senior',
    ],
  },
  {
    name: 'Harbor Studio',
    key: 'HAR',
    domain: 'harbor.example',
    color: 'green',
    rows: [
      'Priya Nair,priya@tidewater.example,COO,Tidewater Energy,tidewater.example,Ready,TEAMMATE,High,executive',
      'Mateo Rossi,mateo@larkspur.example,Head of Product,Larkspur Foods,larkspur.example,Contacted,OWNER,Medium,senior',
      'Ingrid Larsen,ingrid@cobalt.example,Founder,Cobalt Security,cobalt.example,Meeting held,TEAMMATE,Urgent,executive',
      'Tomas Novak,tomas@meridian.example,Product Designer,Meridian Travel,meridian.example,Qualified,OWNER,Low,junior',
      'Amara Okafor,amara@vela.example,VP Engineering,Vela Robotics,vela.example,Researching,TEAMMATE,Medium,executive',
    ],
  },
  {
    name: 'Atlas Market',
    key: 'ATL',
    domain: 'atlasmarket.example',
    color: 'amber',
    rows: [
      'Aisha Bello,aisha@orchard.example,Owner,Orchard Learning,orchard.example,New,OWNER,Medium,senior',
      'Kenji Watanabe,kenji@northwind.example,Operations Lead,Northwind Freight,northwind.example,Contacted,TEAMMATE,Low,senior',
      'Elena Petrova,elena@saltmarsh.example,Founder,Saltmarsh Ceramics,saltmarsh.example,Closed: no reply,OWNER,Low,executive',
      'Noah Fischer,noah@brightloom.example,Shop Manager,Brightloom Textiles,brightloom.example,Replied,TEAMMATE,High,junior',
      'Hana Kim,hana@juniper.example,Analytics Lead,Juniper Bank,juniper.example,New,TEAMMATE,Low,senior',
    ],
  },
  {
    name: 'Seed Round',
    key: 'SED',
    domain: 'seedround.example',
    color: 'violet',
    rows: [
      'Zara Ahmed,zara@halyard.example,Partner,Halyard Ventures,halyard.example,Ready,OWNER,Urgent,executive',
      'Lucas Moreau,lucas@pinecrest.example,Principal,Pinecrest Partners,pinecrest.example,Contacted,OWNER,High,senior',
      'Maya Cohen,maya@granite.example,Angel,Granite Angels,granite.example,Meeting booked,OWNER,Medium,executive',
      'Diego Alvarez,diego@larchwood.example,Associate,Larchwood Capital,larchwood.example,Closed: not a fit,OWNER,Low,junior',
    ],
  },
];

const SENIORITY_OPTIONS = [
  { value: 'junior', label: 'Junior' },
  { value: 'senior', label: 'Senior' },
  { value: 'executive', label: 'Executive' },
];

async function ensureUser(name: string, email: string, handle: string) {
  const [existing] = await db.select().from(schema.user).where(eq(schema.user.email, email)).limit(1);
  if (existing !== undefined) return existing;
  const [created] = await db
    .insert(schema.user)
    .values({ id: newId(), name, email, handle, emailVerified: true })
    .returning();
  if (created === undefined) throw new Error(`Could not create the demo user ${email}.`);
  return created;
}

function csvOf(brand: DemoBrand, ownerEmail: string, teammateEmail: string): string {
  const rows = brand.rows.map((row) => row.replace('OWNER', ownerEmail).replace('TEAMMATE', teammateEmail));
  return [HEADER, ...rows].join('\n');
}

export async function seedDemoWorkspace(
  options: DemoSeedOptions = DEMO_SEED_DEFAULTS,
): Promise<DemoSeedResult> {
  const [taken] = await db
    .select({ id: schema.organization.id })
    .from(schema.organization)
    .where(eq(schema.organization.slug, options.slug))
    .limit(1);
  if (taken !== undefined) {
    throw conflict(`A workspace with the slug ${options.slug} already exists. Pass --slug to seed another one.`);
  }
  const owner = await ensureUser('Alex Rivera', `alex@${options.domain}`, `${options.slug}-alex`);
  const teammate = await ensureUser('Sam Okafor', `sam@${options.domain}`, `${options.slug}-sam`);
  const created = await createOrganization(owner.id, { name: 'Demo Outreach', slug: options.slug });
  const organizationId = created.organization.id;
  await db
    .insert(schema.member)
    .values({ id: newId(), organizationId, userId: teammate.id, role: 'member' })
    .onConflictDoNothing();
  const context = { principal: await resolvePrincipal(owner.id, organizationId) };
  await createFieldDefinition(context, {
    object: 'person',
    key: 'seniority',
    label: 'Seniority',
    type: 'select',
    options: SENIORITY_OPTIONS,
  });
  let leads = 0;
  for (const brand of DEMO_BRANDS) {
    const made = await createBrand(context, {
      name: brand.name,
      domain: brand.domain,
      color: brand.color,
      pipelineKey: brand.key,
    });
    const report = await commitImport(
      context,
      {
        format: 'csv',
        content: csvOf(brand, owner.email, teammate.email),
        target: 'leads',
        pipelineId: made.pipeline.id,
        mapping: SEED_MAPPING,
        source: 'demo-seed',
        defaultOwner: 'me',
      },
      { actorName: owner.name },
    );
    const problems = report.rows.flatMap((row) => row.issues.map((issue) => `row ${issue.row}: ${issue.message}`));
    if (report.status !== 'completed' || problems.length > 0) {
      throw new Error(`The ${brand.name} seed did not import cleanly: ${report.failure?.message ?? problems.join('; ')}`);
    }
    leads += report.totals.leadsCreated;
  }
  const people = await db.$count(schema.person, eq(schema.person.organizationId, organizationId));
  return {
    organizationId,
    slug: options.slug,
    ownerEmail: owner.email,
    teammateEmail: teammate.email,
    brands: DEMO_BRANDS.length,
    people,
    leads,
  };
}
```

`packages/core/src/seed/run-seed.ts`:

```ts
import { parseDomainList } from '@gravity/shared/utils';
import { closeRealtime } from '../realtime/publisher.ts';
import { DEMO_SEED_DEFAULTS, seedDemoWorkspace } from './demo-seed.ts';

function flag(name: string): string | undefined {
  const index = process.argv.indexOf(`--${name}`);
  return index === -1 ? undefined : process.argv[index + 1];
}

const options = {
  slug: flag('slug') ?? DEMO_SEED_DEFAULTS.slug,
  domain: flag('domain') ?? DEMO_SEED_DEFAULTS.domain,
};

let code = 0;
try {
  const result = await seedDemoWorkspace(options);
  console.info(`Seeded ${result.slug}: ${result.brands} brands, ${result.people} people, ${result.leads} leads.`);
  console.info(`Sign in as ${result.ownerEmail} or ${result.teammateEmail} with the dev sign-in.`);
  const allowed = parseDomainList(process.env['ALLOWED_EMAIL_DOMAINS'], 'ALLOWED_EMAIL_DOMAINS');
  if (allowed.length > 0 && !allowed.includes(options.domain)) {
    console.info(
      `ALLOWED_EMAIL_DOMAINS does not include ${options.domain}, so these users cannot sign in. Add it, or seed with --domain <an allowed domain>.`,
    );
  }
} catch (error: unknown) {
  console.error(error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error));
  code = 1;
}
await closeRealtime();
process.exit(code);
```

Append `export * from './seed/demo-seed.ts';` to `packages/core/src/index.ts`. In the root `package.json` add `"db:seed": "bun --env-file=.env packages/core/src/seed/run-seed.ts"`. In `README.md` under Develop add: `bun run db:seed` creates a demo workspace (`--slug` and `--domain` change it).

Run: `cd packages/core && bun test tests/seed && bun run typecheck`
Expected: PASS (`people` is two fewer than `leads`, because two people are pursued by two brands).

Expected: `Seeded demo: 4 brands, 18 people, 20 leads.` Sign in through the dev sign-in and check that the four brands, their pipelines and the shared people appear.

Run: `bun run lint:fix && bun run verify`
Expected: green.

### Task 17: End to end: an agent connects over OAuth, and an import reaches a teammate

Two Playwright tests against the running dev stack, in the style of `apps/web/e2e/realtime-lead.spec.ts`. The first walks the whole MCP chain a real client walks: 401 with resource metadata, discovery, dynamic registration, `/api/oauth/start` with PKCE and the resource, the consent page in a signed-in browser, the code exchange, `initialize`, `tools/list`, a `search` call that finds the seeded person, then revocation from Settings and a 401. The second imports a CSV from the import page, checks a teammate's open list receives the rows without reading `/api/leads`, and checks a second run adds nothing.

**Files:**
- Modify: `apps/web/e2e/fixture.ts`, `apps/web/e2e/global-setup.ts`, `apps/web/e2e/README.md`
- Create: `apps/web/e2e/mcp-oauth.spec.ts`, `apps/web/e2e/import.spec.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: everything from Tasks 4 to 15; `createOrganization`, `createInvite`, `acceptInvite`, `createBrand`, `upsertPerson`, `resolvePrincipal` (core); `createUser` (core test support).
- Produces: fixture fields `workspaceName: string` and `personName: string`.


In `apps/web/e2e/global-setup.ts` add `upsertPerson` to the `@gravity/core` import, and after the brand is created:


- [ ] **Step 2: Write the OAuth test**

`apps/web/e2e/mcp-oauth.spec.ts`:

```ts
import { createHash, randomBytes } from 'node:crypto';
import { type APIRequestContext, expect, test } from '@playwright/test';
import { BASE } from './base-url.ts';
import { readFixture } from './fixture.ts';

const CALLBACK = 'http://127.0.0.1:47823/callback';
const MCP_HEADERS = {
  accept: 'application/json, text/event-stream',
  'content-type': 'application/json',
  'mcp-protocol-version': '2025-06-18',
};

interface RpcAnswer {
  readonly result?: Record<string, unknown>;
  readonly error?: { readonly message: string };
}

function pkce(): { verifier: string; challenge: string } {
  const verifier = randomBytes(32).toString('base64url');
  return { verifier, challenge: createHash('sha256').update(verifier).digest('base64url') };
}

async function rpc(
  request: APIRequestContext,
  token: string,
  id: number,
  method: string,
  params: Record<string, unknown>,
): Promise<RpcAnswer> {
  const response = await request.post(`${BASE}/mcp`, {
    headers: { ...MCP_HEADERS, authorization: `Bearer ${token}` },
    data: { jsonrpc: '2.0', id, method, params },
  });
  expect(response.status()).toBe(200);
  return (await response.json()) as RpcAnswer;
}

test('an agent connects over OAuth, searches the workspace and loses access when revoked', async ({
  browser,
  playwright,
}) => {
  const fixture = readFixture();
  const context = await browser.newContext();
  const signedIn = await context.request.post(`${BASE}/api/dev/sign-in`, { data: { email: fixture.ownerEmail } });
  expect(signedIn.ok()).toBe(true);
  const agent = await playwright.request.newContext();

  const anonymous = await agent.post(`${BASE}/mcp`, {
    headers: MCP_HEADERS,
    data: { jsonrpc: '2.0', id: 0, method: 'tools/list', params: {} },
  });
  expect(anonymous.status()).toBe(401);
  expect(anonymous.headers()['www-authenticate']).toContain('/.well-known/oauth-protected-resource/mcp');

  const metadata = (await (await agent.get(`${BASE}/.well-known/oauth-authorization-server`)).json()) as {
    authorization_endpoint: string;
    token_endpoint: string;
    registration_endpoint: string;
  };
  expect(metadata.authorization_endpoint).toContain('/api/oauth/start');
  const registered = await agent.post(metadata.registration_endpoint, {
    data: {
      client_name: 'E2E agent',
      redirect_uris: [CALLBACK],
      token_endpoint_auth_method: 'none',
      grant_types: ['authorization_code', 'refresh_token'],
      response_types: ['code'],
    },
  });
  expect(registered.ok()).toBe(true);
  const { client_id: clientId } = (await registered.json()) as { client_id: string };

  const { verifier, challenge } = pkce();
  const page = await context.newPage();
  let code: string | null = null;
  await page.route(`${CALLBACK}**`, async (route) => {
    code = new URL(route.request().url()).searchParams.get('code');
    await route.fulfill({ status: 200, contentType: 'text/plain', body: 'Connected' });
  });
  const authorize = new URL(metadata.authorization_endpoint);
  authorize.search = new URLSearchParams({
    response_type: 'code',
    client_id: clientId,
    redirect_uri: CALLBACK,
    scope: 'openid offline_access gravity.read',
    state: 'e2e-state',
    code_challenge: challenge,
    code_challenge_method: 'S256',
    resource: `${BASE}/mcp`,
  }).toString();
  await page.goto(authorize.toString());
  await expect(page.getByRole('heading', { name: 'Connect to Gravity' })).toBeVisible();
  await page.getByLabel('Workspace').selectOption({ label: fixture.workspaceName });
  await page.keyboard.press('ControlOrMeta+Enter');
  await expect.poll(() => code).not.toBeNull();

  const exchanged = await agent.post(metadata.token_endpoint, {
    form: {
      grant_type: 'authorization_code',
      code: code ?? '',
      redirect_uri: CALLBACK,
      client_id: clientId,
      code_verifier: verifier,
    },
  });
  expect(exchanged.ok()).toBe(true);
  const { access_token: accessToken } = (await exchanged.json()) as { access_token: string };

  const initialized = await rpc(agent, accessToken, 1, 'initialize', {
    protocolVersion: '2025-06-18',
    capabilities: {},
    clientInfo: { name: 'gravity-e2e', version: '0.0.0' },
  });
  expect(initialized.result?.['serverInfo']).toMatchObject({ name: 'gravity' });
  const listed = await rpc(agent, accessToken, 2, 'tools/list', {});
  const names = ((listed.result?.['tools'] ?? []) as { name: string }[]).map((tool) => tool.name);
  expect(names).toEqual(expect.arrayContaining(['describe_workspace', 'search', 'get_context', 'list_leads']));
  const searched = await rpc(agent, accessToken, 3, 'tools/call', {
    name: 'search',
    arguments: { query: fixture.personName },
  });
  const people = (searched.result?.['structuredContent'] as { people: { name: string; url: string }[] }).people;
  expect(people[0]?.name).toBe(fixture.personName);
  expect(people[0]?.url).toContain('/people/');

  await page.goto(`${BASE}/settings/mcp`);
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Revoke E2E agent' }).click();
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Revoke access' }).click();
  await expect(page.getByRole('listitem', { name: 'E2E agent' })).toHaveCount(0);
  const refused = await agent.post(`${BASE}/mcp`, {
    headers: { ...MCP_HEADERS, authorization: `Bearer ${accessToken}` },
    data: { jsonrpc: '2.0', id: 4, method: 'tools/list', params: {} },
  });
  expect(refused.status()).toBe(401);
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the import test**

`apps/web/e2e/import.spec.ts`:

```ts
import { type BrowserContext, expect, type Page, test } from '@playwright/test';
import { BASE } from './base-url.ts';
import { readFixture } from './fixture.ts';

async function signIn(context: BrowserContext, email: string): Promise<Page> {
  const response = await context.request.post(`${BASE}/api/dev/sign-in`, { data: { email } });
  if (!response.ok()) {
    throw new Error(`Dev sign-in for ${email} failed with ${response.status()}: ${await response.text()}.`);
  }
  return await context.newPage();
}

test('an import previews, reaches a teammate by delta, and a second run adds nothing', async ({ browser }) => {
  const fixture = readFixture();
  const owner = await signIn(await browser.newContext(), fixture.ownerEmail);
  const teammate = await signIn(await browser.newContext(), fixture.teammateEmail);
  await teammate.goto(`${BASE}/leads/${fixture.pipelineKey}`);
  await teammate.waitForLoadState('networkidle');
  const reads: string[] = [];
  teammate.on('request', (request) => {
    if (request.method() === 'GET' && new URL(request.url()).pathname.startsWith('/api/leads')) {
      reads.push(request.url());
    }
  });

  const suffix = Date.now().toString(36);
  const names = [`Imported One ${suffix}`, `Imported Two ${suffix}`, `Imported Three ${suffix}`];
  const csv = [
    'Full Name,Work Email,Company,Website,Stage',
    `${names[0]},one-${suffix}@vela.example,Vela Robotics,vela.example,Ready`,
    `${names[1]},two-${suffix}@quarry.example,Quarry Labs,quarry.example,New`,
    `${names[2]},three-${suffix}@kestrel.example,,,Contacted`,
  ].join('\n');
  const file = { name: 'leads.csv', mimeType: 'text/csv', buffer: Buffer.from(csv) };

  await owner.goto(`${BASE}/import?target=leads&pipeline=${fixture.pipelineKey}`);
  await owner.getByLabel('Import file').setInputFiles(file);
  await expect(owner.getByLabel('Field for Full Name')).toHaveValue('person.name');
  await expect(owner.getByLabel('Field for Website')).toHaveValue('company.domain');
  await owner.keyboard.press('ControlOrMeta+Enter');
  await expect(owner.getByText('3 new', { exact: true })).toBeVisible();
  await owner.keyboard.press('ControlOrMeta+Enter');
  await expect(owner.getByText('Imported 3 rows')).toBeVisible();
  for (const name of names) {
    await expect(teammate.locator('[data-testid^="lead-row-"]', { hasText: name })).toBeVisible();
  }
  expect(reads).toEqual([]);

  await owner.getByRole('button', { name: 'Import another file' }).click();
  await owner.getByLabel('Import file').setInputFiles(file);
  await owner.keyboard.press('ControlOrMeta+Enter');
  await expect(owner.getByText('3 unchanged', { exact: true })).toBeVisible();
  await expect(owner.getByText('3 leads already open', { exact: true })).toBeVisible();
});
```

```md
The MCP test drives the OAuth consent page, which only accepts a decision from the
page's own origin, so `NEXT_PUBLIC_APP_URL` in the web server's environment must be
the same origin as `GRAVITY_E2E_BASE_URL` (both default to `http://localhost:3300`).
It registers an OAuth client named `E2E agent` per run. The import test imports three
leads per run into the fixture pipeline.
```

### Task 18: Performance budgets from the UI spec

UI spec 4: "Budgets, enforced by a Playwright performance test in M0.3: keystroke to visible change under 16ms, route change from cache under 100ms, a teammate's change visible in another browser under 500ms on a local network." The third is already enforced by `realtime-lead.spec.ts`. This task adds the first two, measured inside the page so Playwright's own round trips do not count: a capture-phase `keydown` timestamp to the `MutationObserver` callback that sees the new `data-active` row (keystroke), and to the first record row of the cached People list (route). Each budget is checked against the median of its samples.

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/web/e2e/performance.spec.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `POST /api/imports` (Task 14), the fixture (Task 17), `data-active` on lead rows (M0.2), `data-testid="record-row-*"` on record lists (M0.2), the `G P` and `G L` chords.
- Produces: none.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the test**

`apps/web/e2e/performance.spec.ts`:

```ts
import { type BrowserContext, expect, type Page, test } from '@playwright/test';
import { BASE } from './base-url.ts';
import { readFixture } from './fixture.ts';

const KEYSTROKE_BUDGET_MS = 16;
const ROUTE_BUDGET_MS = 100;
const KEYSTROKE_SAMPLES = 21;
const ROUTE_SAMPLES = 5;
const SEEDED_LEADS = 40;

function median(values: readonly number[]): number {
  const sorted = [...values].sort((left, right) => left - right);
  return sorted[Math.floor(sorted.length / 2)] ?? Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
}

async function signIn(context: BrowserContext, email: string): Promise<Page> {
  const response = await context.request.post(`${BASE}/api/dev/sign-in`, { data: { email } });
  if (!response.ok()) throw new Error(`Dev sign-in failed with ${response.status()}.`);
  return await context.newPage();
}

function installKeystrokeProbe(): void {
  const probe = { started: 0, samples: [] as number[] };
  Object.assign(window, { gravityKeystrokeProbe: probe });
  window.addEventListener(
    'keydown',
    () => {
      probe.started = performance.now();
    },
    { capture: true },
  );
  new MutationObserver((records) => {
    if (probe.started === 0) return;
    const activated = records.some(
      (record) => record.target instanceof Element && record.target.getAttribute('data-active') === 'true',
    );
    if (!activated) return;
    probe.samples.push(performance.now() - probe.started);
    probe.started = 0;
  }).observe(document.body, { attributes: true, subtree: true, attributeFilter: ['data-active'] });
}

function keystrokeSamples(): number[] {
  const holder = window as unknown as { gravityKeystrokeProbe?: { samples: number[] } };
  return holder.gravityKeystrokeProbe?.samples ?? [];
}

function installRouteProbe(): void {
  const probe: { started: number; elapsed: number | null } = { started: 0, elapsed: null };
  Object.assign(window, { gravityRouteProbe: probe });
  const onKey = (event: KeyboardEvent) => {
    if (event.key === 'p') probe.started = performance.now();
  };
  window.addEventListener('keydown', onKey, { capture: true });
  const observer = new MutationObserver(() => {
    if (probe.started === 0 || probe.elapsed !== null) return;
    if (document.querySelector('[data-testid^="record-row-"]') === null) return;
    probe.elapsed = performance.now() - probe.started;
    observer.disconnect();
    window.removeEventListener('keydown', onKey, { capture: true });
  });
  observer.observe(document.body, { childList: true, subtree: true });
}

function routeElapsed(): number | null {
  const holder = window as unknown as { gravityRouteProbe?: { elapsed: number | null } };
  return holder.gravityRouteProbe?.elapsed ?? null;
}

test('keystrokes and cached route changes stay inside the UI spec budgets', async ({ browser }) => {
  const fixture = readFixture();
  const page = await signIn(await browser.newContext(), fixture.ownerEmail);
  const suffix = Date.now().toString(36);
  const rows = Array.from({ length: SEEDED_LEADS }, (_, index) => `Perf ${suffix} ${index},perf-${suffix}-${index}@vela.example`);
  const seeded = await page.request.post(`${BASE}/api/imports`, {
    data: {
      format: 'csv',
      content: ['Name,Email', ...rows].join('\n'),
      target: 'leads',
      pipelineId: fixture.pipelineId,
      mapping: { Name: 'person.name', Email: 'person.email' },
      source: 'perf',
    },
  });
  expect(seeded.ok()).toBe(true);

  await page.goto(`${BASE}/leads/${fixture.pipelineKey}`);
  await expect(page.locator('[data-testid^="lead-row-"]').first()).toBeVisible();
  await page.keyboard.press('j');
  await expect(page.locator('[data-active="true"]')).toHaveCount(1);
  await page.evaluate(installKeystrokeProbe);
  for (let index = 0; index < KEYSTROKE_SAMPLES; index += 1) {
    await page.keyboard.press(index % 2 === 0 ? 'j' : 'k');
    await page.waitForTimeout(40);
  }
  const keystrokes = await page.evaluate(keystrokeSamples);
  expect(keystrokes).toHaveLength(KEYSTROKE_SAMPLES);
  expect(median(keystrokes)).toBeLessThan(KEYSTROKE_BUDGET_MS);

  await page.keyboard.press('g');
  await page.keyboard.press('p');
  await expect(page.locator('[data-testid^="record-row-"]').first()).toBeVisible();
  const routes: number[] = [];
  for (let index = 0; index < ROUTE_SAMPLES; index += 1) {
    await page.keyboard.press('g');
    await page.keyboard.press('l');
    await expect(page.locator('[data-testid^="lead-row-"]').first()).toBeVisible();
    await page.evaluate(installRouteProbe);
    await page.keyboard.press('g');
    await page.keyboard.press('p');
    await expect.poll(() => page.evaluate(routeElapsed)).not.toBeNull();
    routes.push((await page.evaluate(routeElapsed)) ?? Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY);
  }
  expect(median(routes)).toBeLessThan(ROUTE_BUDGET_MS);
});
```

## Self-review

Checked against the specs with this plan written out:

- **Spec coverage.** Design 7 (MCP over OAuth with DCR and PKCE, consent with workspace choice and passkey re-verification, grant-bound tokens validated against the database, scopes deciding tools, 403 before any tool, 401 challenge): Tasks 3 to 6 and 10. Design 7.2 read tools owned by M0: Tasks 6 and 8; deep links in every response: Task 6's `recordLinks`, used by Tasks 7, 8 and 9. Design 3.2 MCP OAuth tables: already in `schema/oauth.ts`; `import_source` added in Task 12. Design 3.3 and UI spec 5 "human edits win": Task 13 (integration actor, kept fields, kept employer and title). Design 9.4 outbox and fan-out: Task 13 commits through `withBatch` per chunk and publishes per chunk; Task 17 proves the teammate receives the rows without a refetch. Design 11 (adapters, idempotent on source ids, dedupe on email, LinkedIn and domain, dry run first): Tasks 11 to 14. Design 12 (tenant scoping in core, policy, fictional seed data): every core read is principal-scoped, Task 8 and Task 13 pin the cross-workspace refusals, Task 16 pins `.example` domains. Design 14 M0 done-when: Tasks 16 and 17. UI spec 4 budgets: Task 18 (and `realtime-lead.spec.ts`). UI spec 5 copy for agent: Task 9. UI spec 7 keys: `Cmd+Enter` on consent and import, `O` and `Esc` on import, `Cmd+Shift+A` on records. UI spec 8 states: empty, delayed skeleton, inline errors that name the fix and the role. The deferred items are listed under Scope rulings.
- **Placeholder scan.** No step says TBD or "similar to". Two steps tell the implementer to adapt to an observed fact rather than guess: the exact zod wording of an invalid email in the CLI test (with the exact fallback assertion), and recording measured medians in Task 18.
- **Type consistency.** `findPersonMatch(executor, organizationId, input, { lock, preferredId })` and `findCompanyMatch(executor, organizationId, input, lock)` are defined in Task 12 and used with those shapes in Task 13. `ImportReport`, `ImportRowOutcome` and `totalsOf` are defined in Task 11 and consumed in Tasks 13, 14 and 15. `commitImport(context, input, { actorName, publish?, limits?, chunkRows? })` matches its callers in Tasks 14 and 16. `mintMcpToken` returns `{ token, clientId, grantId, rawAccessToken }` everywhere. `CONTEXT_TOKENS` and `leadPriorityLabel` come from shared constants in Task 7 and are used in Tasks 8 and 9. `finalizeMcpConsent` takes `allowApproval` in Tasks 3, 4 and 5.
- **Review Focus.** Each of the five lines names its test and owning task, and each test is written out in that task.
